TL;DR: DiaVLo 是一个诊断框架，利用人工标注与 VLM 的生成能力来构建期望/观测行为的规范，从而暴露潜在的不对齐，并提供因果估计以识别驱动 VLM 行为的最具影响力概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）依赖在各子组件间存储和传递恰当信息；验证 VLM 表现出期望行为、避免有害行为是其可靠部署的核心。然而，现有用于识别 VLM 行为的方法仍很匮乏，因此需要一种系统化的诊断手段。

Method: 提出 DiaVLo 诊断框架，结合人工策展（human curation）与 VLM 自身的生成能力，构建期望行为与观测行为的规范（specifications）以揭示潜在的不对齐；此外还提供因果估计，用于识别对 VLM 行为影响最大的概念（concepts）。

Result: 在多个开源 VLM 上、分类与生成两种条件下评估，结果表明 DiaVLo 生成的行为标签与模型表现相关，并为所测性能提供了上下文解释；同时它揭示出明确对齐与不对齐的行为，以及 VLM 如何感知、组织与优先排序概念的模式。

Conclusion: DiaVLo 能有效生成可解释且与性能相关的行为标签，揭示 VLM 内在的对齐/不对齐行为及概念优先排序模式，为 VLM 的可诊断性与可靠部署提供了支持。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) rely on storing and transferring appropriate information across their sub-components. Verifying that the VLMs exhibit desired behaviours, while avoiding harmful ones, is central to their reliable deployment. Yet, methods that identify VLM behaviours remain scarce. We present DiaVLo, a diagnostic framework that leverages human curation and VLMs' generation capabilities to construct specifications of desired and observed VLM behaviours, surfacing potential misalignments. Beyond this, DiaVLo also provides causal estimates to identify the most influential concepts steering VLM behaviours. We evaluate DiaVLo on several open-source VLMs under both classification and generation conditions. Our experiments show that DiaVLo produces behaviour labels that correlate with model performance and provide context for measured performance. DiaVLo surfaced behaviours that are clearly aligned and misaligned, alongside patterns in how VLMs perceive, organise, and prioritise concepts.

</details>


### [138] [QuranicMMLU: A Cognitively-Aware Benchmark for Evaluating Generative AI Solutions on Quranic Linguistic Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22038)
*Rawan El Ghali,Umm Kulsoom,Anas Madkoor,Dima Faris Alsaudi,Roaa Abdelmagid,Roaa Ibrahim,Raghad Mousa,Hamza Aljaji,Abdullah Khanafer,Abdallah Alkanani,Salah Feras Alali,Rawan Khaled Mohamed,Ehsaneddin Asgari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 QuranicMMLU，一个按语音学、形态学、句法学、语义学、语用学五大支柱构建的古兰经阿拉伯语生成式 AI 评测基准，包含 980 道经人工审核的题目，并发现多选题形式会掩盖模型真实能力的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有古兰经测评基准仅聚焦于通用问答和语义检索，未深入探测具体语言能力，也未按认知需求和经文难度进行分层，因此无法精细评估模型在古兰经阿拉伯语上的真实语言学水平。

Method: 构建覆盖语音学、形态学、句法学、语义学、语用学的五大支柱分类体系，共 31 个叶子节点（涵盖诵读规则、词根-词式形态、降示背景、章间连贯等现象）；对每个叶子按布鲁姆认知层级与经文困惑度分层生成题目，由 LLM 作为裁判独立作答并打分，再转入人工审核，最终形成 980 道经人工复核的题目，每题同时提供开放式与多选题两种形式。

Result: 对 12 个系统进行评测：伊斯兰专用模型表现最佳；所有系统在多选题准确率（平均 84%）上均高于开放式回答质量（平均 60%）；两种排序高度一致（Kendall's τ=0.73），但多选评分掩盖了仅在移除选项后才暴露的缺陷。

Conclusion: QuranicMMLU 为古兰经领域的阿拉伯语 NLP 提供了一个严谨、有语言学依据的评测框架，并揭示多选题可能高估模型能力、开放式评测更能暴露真实缺陷。

Abstract: We introduce QuranicMMLU, a benchmark for evaluating generative AI on Quranic Arabic across multiple dimensions of linguistic complexity. Existing Quranic benchmarks center on general question answering and semantic retrieval, without probing specific linguistic competencies or stratifying by cognitive demand and verse difficulty. We construct a five-pillar Quranic taxonomy spanning Phonology, Morphology, Syntax, Semantics, and Pragmatics, with 31 leaves covering phenomena from tajwīd and root-and-pattern morphology to occasions of revelation and inter-surah coherence. For each leaf we generate questions stratified by Bloom's cognitive level and verse perplexity, then have LLM as a judge to independently answer and score every item and route the annotations to manual review. The resulting dataset comprises 980 human-reviewed questions, each issued in both open-ended and multiple-choice form. We benchmark 12 systems on these items and find that the Islamic-specialized model leads, yet every system scores higher on multiple-choice accuracy (average 84%) than open-ended answer quality (average 60%): the two rankings agree closely (Kendall's τ=0.73), but multiple-choice scoring hides failures that surface only once answer choices are removed. QuranicMMLU thus offers a rigorous, linguistically grounded framework for evaluating Arabic NLP in the Quranic domain.

</details>


### [139] [An Interpretable Memory Decision Controller for LLM Agents Based on Three-Signal Complementarity: Decoupling Confidence and Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22043)
*Yiming Zhang,Jinghong Zhang,Haoran Zhao,Yiren Ma,Chunlei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零参数的记忆决策层（MDL），置于检索与生成之间，通过三信号互补编码器（相关性、可靠性、任务风险）判断检索记忆是否可信，可将冲突记忆下的幻觉率降低约56.04%，高风险场景近乎归零，且决策开销仅约0.14ms、无训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统偏重检索效率，却忽视“检索到的记忆是否可信”这一决策问题。当记忆库含冲突观点时，标准RAG盲注记忆会放大幻觉，使幻觉率显著高于无记忆基线。受前额叶皮层记忆信号机制启发，作者希望为检索与生成之间引入显式的记忆信任决策。

Method: 提出记忆决策层MDL：一个位于检索与生成阶段之间的零参数记忆决策控制器。核心为三信号互补编码器，将相关性、可靠性与任务风险三个信号，通过基于QR分解的正交子空间投影及元工作记忆信号，融合为可解释的决策表示，量化检索记忆的可信度。在此基础上，MDL显式解耦置信度与一致性，并引入风险反转与显式弃权机制。MDL完全白盒，仅用几何运算、无训练参数。

Result: 在主流LLM和多个开源数据集上评估，MDL在一般场景下将冲突记忆下的幻觉率降低约56.04%，高风险场景下幻觉率趋近于零。控制器每次决策仅约0.14ms，约比前置的嵌入检索步骤快50倍，比LLM自评估调用快四到五个数量级。

Conclusion: MDL通过无参数的几何编码与显式信任决策，有效解决了冲突记忆下的幻觉放大问题，在保持极低推理开销的前提下显著提升可靠性，并具备完全白盒、可解释、无需训练的优势。

Abstract: Memory systems for large language models have focused predominantly on efficient retrieval, whereas the decision of whether retrieved memories should be trusted has received comparatively little attention. When the memory store contains conflicting positions, standard retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) blindly injects memories and amplifies hallucinations: in models susceptible to memory injection, the RAG hallucination rate under conflicting memories is markedly higher than that of a memory-free baseline. Inspired by memory signaling mechanisms in the prefrontal cortex, we propose the Memory Decision Layer (MDL), a zero-parameter memory decision controller situated between the retrieval and generation stages. Its core is a three-signal complementary encoder that fuses relevance, reliability, and task risk through QR-based orthogonal subspace projection and a meta-working-memory signal into an interpretable decision representation that quantifies the trustworthiness of retrieved memories. Building on this encoder, MDL explicitly decouples confidence from consistency and introduces risk inversion and explicit abstention. Evaluations on mainstream large language models and multiple open-source datasets show that MDL reduces the hallucination rate under conflicting memories by about 56.04% in general scenarios and approaches zero hallucination in high-risk scenarios. The controller is fully white-box: it relies purely on geometric operations, requires no trained parameters, and adds only about 0.14 ms per decision -- roughly 50x faster than the embedding-retrieval step that precedes it and four to five orders of magnitude faster than an LLM self-evaluation call.

</details>


### [140] [Cross-sector generalization of accident-process role classification in occupational accident narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22081)
*Aho Yapi,Pierre Latouche,Arnaud Guillin,Yan Bailly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估法语职业事故叙述中"事故过程角色分类"的跨行业泛化能力。作者构建专家标注语料，将事实单元分为四类角色（A0工作情境、A1不利条件、B事故事件/偏差、C后果），分类器仅在建筑行业数据上训练，随后在冶金、化工-塑料三个目标域上零样本评估。结果表明，任务特定自适应（微调/监督表征学习）相比冻结表征能持续提升跨域迁移，三种领先策略的平均平衡准确率达85.6%–85.8%，支持构建可迁移的辅助编码系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业事故叙述蕴含工作情境、不利条件、事故事件及后果等有价值信息，自动结构化可支持大规模事故分析与风险预防。但不同行业和组织的术语与写作风格差异巨大，自动编码系统能否泛化到训练域之外存疑，亟需验证跨行业迁移的可行性。

Method: 构建专家标注的法语语料，将事实单元分为四类角色：A0、A1、B、C。角色分类器仅在42,244个事实单元（源自6,040篇建筑行业叙述）上开发与选择，然后在冶金、化工-塑料行业以及独立公司语料上不经重训练或目标域调优地评估。对比冻结预训练表征、任务特定微调与监督表征学习三种策略，并进行多次重复训练以考察稳定性。

Result: 任务特定自适应策略一致优于冻结表征，跨域迁移性能提升明显。在多次重复训练中，三种领先的任务自适应策略在三个目标语料上取得平均平衡准确率85.6%–85.8%。

Conclusion: 研究证实了任务特定自适应对跨域迁移的有效性，支持开发可迁移的辅助编码系统，能够一致地结构化异构的职业事故叙述，供专家审核并服务于跨行业预防分析。

Abstract: Occupational accident narratives contain valuable information about work situations, unfavourable conditions, accident events, and their consequences. Automatically structuring these narratives can facilitate large-scale accident analysis and support occupational risk prevention. However, the terminology and writing styles used to describe accidents vary considerably across sectors and organisations, raising questions about the ability of automated coding systems to generalize beyond their training domain. In this paper, we evaluate the cross-sector generalization of accident-process role classification in French occupational accident narratives. We construct an expert-annotated corpus in which factual units are classified into four roles: work situation (A0), explicitly reported unfavourable condition (A1), accident event or deviation (B), and reported consequence (C). The role classifiers are developed and selected exclusively on 42,244 factual units extracted from 6,040 construction-sector narratives and are then evaluated on unseen corpora from the metallurgy and chemistry--plastics sectors, as well as on an independently collected company corpus, without retraining or target-domain tuning of the role classifier. We compare frozen pretrained representations with task-specific fine-tuning and supervised representation-learning strategies. The results show that task-specific adaptation consistently improves cross-domain transfer over frozen representations. Across repeated training runs, the three leading task-adapted strategies achieved average balanced accuracies between 85.6% and 85.8% across the three target corpora. These findings support the development of transferable assisted-coding systems capable of consistently structuring heterogeneous occupational accident narratives for expert review and cross-sector prevention analysis.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Extreme classification: beating chance with one training example from each class](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20897)
*Kevin Bleakley,Aaditya Ramdas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究一个极简分类问题：在两未知分布 P、Q 等概率生成目标 Y 的设定下，当且仅当 P≠Q 时能否严格优于随机猜测。作者构造了基于 MMD 的核规则、实数线上的确定性序规则，并通过可测编码推广到任意可数生成可测空间；同时证明在未知非均衡先验及自适应目标选择下，除公平硬币外任何规则都会对某有限支撑分布对严格劣于随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究分布无关（distribution-free）分类的最小充分条件：在完全未知分布 P、Q 且目标以等概率来自两分布时，是否存在一个通用规则，使得只要两分布不同（P≠Q）就能严格优于随机猜测，从而刻画"分类优于机会"在无任何分布假设下的可行边界。

Method: 作者依次给出几类规则：一、最近邻规则在多元高斯（均值不同、公共正定协方差）下成功，但对实数线上的光滑密度可严格劣于随机；二、构造固定随机化核规则，期望准确率恰为 1/2 + MMD_k²(P,Q)/4，并借助可数族可测二值问题在可数生成可测空间上得到特征核；三、证明实数线上确定性序规则对任意相异 Borel 概率测度对均优于随机；四、通过可测编码将序规则推广为可数生成可测空间（含一切可分度量空间）上的确定性分布无关规则；五、针对未知非均衡类先验与自适应目标选择，分析其不可能性。

Result: 主要结果包括：随机化核规则的期望准确率精确公式 1/2 + MMD_k²(P,Q)/4 及其特征核构造；实数线上确定性序规则对任意相异 Borel 概率测度对均严格优于随机；经过可测编码得到适用于一切可数生成可测空间（特别是所有可分度量空间）的确定性分布无关规则；同时在未知非均衡先验和自适应目标类选择下，任何非"公平硬币"式规则都会在某有限支撑分布对上严格劣于随机，即等概率先验的假设不可去除。

Conclusion: 在目标等概率来自两未知分布且无需任何分布假设的条件下，存在确定性分布无关规则，在一切可数生成可测空间中实现"只要 P≠Q 就严格优于随机"；该结果通过 MMD 核规则与序规则的可测编码统一达成。然而一旦放弃等概率先验并引入自适应目标选择，除公平硬币外不存在任何通用规则，揭示等概率先验是分类可判别性的关键条件。

Abstract: We study a minimal classification problem: Given independent labeled observations $X\sim P$ and $Z\sim Q$ from two unknown distributions $P,Q$, and given an independent target $Y$ drawn with equal probability from $P$ or $Q$, can one classify $Y$ strictly better than chance whenever $P\neq Q$? The one-nearest-neighbor rule succeeds for every pair of multivariate Gaussian distributions with distinct means and a common positive-definite covariance matrix but can perform strictly worse than chance even for smooth densities on the real line. We construct a fixed randomized kernel rule whose expected accuracy is exactly $1/2+\operatorname{MMD}_k^2(P,Q)/4$, and obtain characteristic kernels on countably generated measurable spaces from countable families of measurable binary questions. We also prove that a deterministic order rule on $\mathbb R$ beats chance for every pair of distinct Borel probability measures. A measurable encoding then gives a deterministic distribution-free rule which beats chance on every countably generated measurable space, in particular every separable metric space. Finally, we show that no rule works for every distinct pair of distributions and every unknown unbalanced class prior; under adaptive target-class selection, every rule other than a fair coin is strictly worse than chance for some finitely supported pair.

</details>


### [142] [Complex Problem Solving in Large Language Models: A Statistical Control Survey and Diagnostic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20973)
*Jiazhang Cai,Tao Wang,Ruidong Zhang,Siyuan Li,Terry Ma,Luyang Fang,Haoran Lu,Huimin Cheng,Yingchuan Zhang,Shushan Wu,Rui Xie,Lin Tang,Chao Huang,Rongjie Liu,Ziyu Liu,Meizhi Yu,Yongkai Chen,Yifan Zhou,Zeliang Sun,Chang Liu,Zhen Xiang,Wei Xiao,Zixin Rao,Xinyi Liu,Yutong Hu,Mengrui Zhang,Jing Zhang,Weidi Luo,Jincheng Yu,Zhengliang Liu,Weihang You,Hanqi Jiang,Yi Pan,Junhao Chen,Xinliang Li,Tianming Liu,Wenxuan Zhong,Ping Ma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本综述将大语言模型的复杂问题求解重新理解为对潜在解状态进行"序贯估计与决策"的过程，提出"控制器"视角，并围绕五个组件组织现有方法，进而引出"问题-控制匹配"（problem-control fit）这一诊断假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM复杂问题求解（CPS）的解释多归结为"推理能力不足"或"生成长度不够"，但早期步骤的误差放大、提示词脆弱性、以及无法修正错误承诺等现象，难以仅用知识缺失或表达容量不足来解释，因此需要一个统一框架来理解并诊断这些失败。

Method: 将CPS建模为对潜在（不可直接观测）解轨迹的序贯估计与决策问题：控制器在接收带噪中间证据时持续更新对解状态的信念，并决定提交、验证、分支、回滚或弃权，以最小化期望损失。推理负责提供候选状态转移与解释，过程控制负责塑造和评估这些提议并调节后续转移与观测。围绕该框架将现有方法归纳为五个组件：显式状态表示、转移构建、验证与约束执行、搜索与回滚、不确定性管理，并将评估指标诠释为其所估计的统计量。

Result: 该框架澄清了当前方法"估计与控制了什么、未控制什么"，并将可靠性验证、定向恢复、校准的不确定性和匹配预算的评估确定为核心开放问题；同时提出诊断假说：当干预针对失败所隐含的误差或不确定性组件时效果最佳。

Conclusion: CPS应被视为估计与控制的耦合问题而非单纯的能力或长度问题；区分系统性、随机性与不可约误差以及认知与偶然不确定性，并提出"问题-控制匹配/控制失配"概念来解释干预为何有效或失效，从而为未来方法设计提供诊断性框架。

Abstract: Complex problem solving (CPS) with large language models (LLMs) is often framed as a matter of stronger reasoning or longer generation. Yet early-step error amplification, prompt brittleness, and failures to revise incorrect commitments are difficult to explain by missing knowledge or expressive capacity alone. This survey interprets CPS as a sequential estimation-and-decision problem over a latent solution state. A controller maintains a belief about an unobserved solution trajectory, updates it as noisy intermediate evidence arrives, and decides whether to commit, verify, branch, roll back, or abstain to minimize expected loss. Reasoning supplies candidate transitions and interpretations, whereas process control shapes and evaluates those proposals and regulates subsequent transitions and observations. Within this framework, we organize existing methods around five components: explicit state representation, transition structuring, validation and constraint enforcement, search and rollback, and uncertainty management. We also interpret evaluation metrics according to the statistical quantities they estimate. The framework further yields a diagnostic hypothesis: interventions should be most effective when they target the error or uncertainty component implicated by an observed failure. We distinguish systematic, stochastic, and irreducible error together with epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty, and call this alignment problem-control fit and its failure control mismatch. For example, additional sampling may reduce sampling variability while leaving a shared systematic error unchanged. This perspective clarifies what current methods estimate and control, what remains uncontrolled, and why reliable validation, targeted recovery, calibrated uncertainty, and matched-budget evaluation are central open problems.

</details>


### [143] [A Smoothed Discrepancy Principle for Random Feature Methods and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21017)
*Mike Nguyen,Nicole Mücke*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在经典非参数回归中研究谱正则化方法的数据驱动提前停止，提出基于偏差原理的多尺度停止规则，并借助随机特征近似在保持极小极大最优保证的同时降低大数据的计算代价，最终给出网络宽度与停止时间的联合数据驱动选择，使神经网络无需先验知识即可达到极小极大最优学习率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提前停止规则通常无法在良设定情形下对所有光滑度水平实现完全自适应，也无法处理大数据下的计算开销与神经网络宽度选择问题。作者希望提出兼具自适应性与计算效率、且能为神经网络容量（宽度）提供理论依据的停止规则。

Method: 在偏差原理基础上构造适用于一般核估计量的多尺度停止规则；通过随机特征近似降低大数据上的计算成本，并给出随机特征数目的数据驱动选择；利用随机特征与神经正切核（NTK）状态下神经网络的联系，将结果转化为网络宽度的原则性推荐，并联合选择宽度与停止时间。

Result: 所提多尺度停止规则在良设定情形下对所有光滑度水平实现完全自适应，优于以往方法；基于随机特征的扩展在保持极小极大最优统计保证的同时显著降低计算开销；同时选定的宽度与停止时间使神经网络无需光滑度或容量参数的先验知识即可获得极小极大最优学习率。

Conclusion: 提出的数据驱动停止规则兼具完全自适应性与计算可扩展性，并首次给出随机特征数量与神经网络宽度的原则性联合选择，证明了神经网络在无先验参数知识下可达到极小极大最优学习率，为高维大数据回归与网络结构选择提供了理论与实用指导。

Abstract: We study data-driven early stopping for spectral regularisation methods in the classical non-parametric regression setting. Building on the discrepancy principle, we propose a multi-scale stopping rule that applies to general kernel estimators and show that, unlike previous approaches, it achieves full adaptivity over all smoothness levels in the well-specified case. A key contribution of our work is an extension based on random feature approximations, which reduces computational cost on large datasets while preserving minimax-optimal statistical guarantees. Our procedure not only selects an optimal stopping time but also provides a fully data-driven choice of the number of random features needed to achieve optimal rates. Through the established connection between random features and neural networks in the neural tangent kernel regime, our method further yields a principled, data-driven recommendation for the network width. We prove that the resulting simultaneously chosen width and stopping time allow neural networks to attain minimax-optimal learning rates without prior knowledge of smoothness or capacity parameters.

</details>


### [144] [Triply-Scalable Equivariant Gaussian Process Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21085)
*Tim Steinert,David Ginsbourger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究等变高斯过程（GPs），将GP的等变性与条件作用相结合，构建了等变稀疏变分高斯过程（SVGP），并提出三元可扩展等变GP，用于SO(2)等变向量场和分子性质预测，在大幅降低计算成本的同时保持准确且具有不确定性量化的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程能提供原则性的概率预测并编码先验知识（包括等变性），但其在大规模科学问题中的应用受限于计算成本；等变神经网络虽然常用，却通常缺乏GP所提供的不确定性量化能力，而这类不确定性在分子研究等应用中很有价值。此外，高维输入和大型对称群进一步对可扩展性提出要求。因此，作者希望在大规模、高维、大对称群场景下获得兼具等变性与不确定性量化的高效GP推断。

Method: 作者首先建立了GP等变性与条件作用之间相互作用的理论结果，并据此通过合适的均值函数和协方差核获得等变稀疏GP。以一类灵活的无积分等变核实例化该框架，得到可扩展且数据高效的GP推断，并引入三元可扩展等变GP。在此基础上，采用等变稀疏变分高斯过程处理SO(2)等变向量场和分子性质预测；同时开发了无矩阵（matrix-free）等变全GP实现，将精确Kronecker约简与预条件共轭梯度求解相结合，实现快速可扩展的全联合预测密度评估；并比较了等变稀疏GP模型中诱导点选择的不同方法。

Result: 在合成SO(2)等变场以及基于量子化学模拟的N-甲基甲酰胺电偶极矩预测中，所提方法在仅为经典GP推断一小部分计算成本的前提下，实现了准确且具备不确定性量化的预测。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析GP等变性与条件作用的关系，并利用合适均值函数和核构建等变稀疏GP，可获得兼具可扩展性、数据效率与不确定性量化的等变GP推断；无矩阵等变全GP实现进一步提升了可扩展性，实验验证了其在SO(2)等变场和分子性质预测上的有效性与低成本。

Abstract: Gaussian processes (GPs) provide principled probabilistic predictions while encoding prior knowledge, including equivariances. Yet, their use in large-scale scientific problems is limited by computational cost. Equivariant neural networks are common but typically lack the uncertainty quantification offered by GPs, which is valuable in applications such as molecular research. High-dimensional inputs and large symmetry groups further demand scalability. We establish results pertaining to the interplay of GP equivariance and conditioning and leverage them to obtain equivariant sparse GPs through suitable mean functions and covariance kernels. We instantiate this framework with a flexible class of integration-free equivariant kernels, yielding scalable and data-efficient GP inference. In particular, we introduce triply scalable equivariant Gaussian processes.
  We employ equivariant sparse variational Gaussian processes for $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant vector fields and molecular property prediction. Alongside the SVGP, we develop a matrix-free equivariant full-GP implementation that combines an exact Kronecker reduction with preconditioned conjugate-gradient solves, enabling fast and scalable evaluation of the full joint predictive density. We further compare different approaches for selecting inducing points in the equivariant sparse GP models. Our test cases include synthetic $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-equivariant fields as well as the prediction of electric dipole moments of N-methylformamide based on quantum chemistry simulations, achieving accurate, uncertainty-aware predictions at a fraction of the computational cost of classical GP inference.

</details>


### [145] [Diagonalized Attention for Individualized Regression: Latent-Row Localization and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21320)
*Borui Peng,Liwei Lin,Feifei Wang,Long Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对矩阵值协变量的"个性化稀疏回归"框架，通过"对角化注意力机制"为每个样本定位各自的感兴趣行（信号行），同时共享回归效应，并从理论上证明了单头与多头注意力在适当条件下能以高概率恢复潜在行并给出预测风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代文本/图像表示常为矩阵值（行对应 token、patch 等），预测信号往往稀疏但随样本而异，传统公共支撑的稀疏回归方法无法适应这种异质性。

Method: 提出对角化注意力机制：利用 query-key 分数定位每个样本特有的信号行，再用 value 矩阵做下游回归；参数维度与样本量无关，且能对新观测在无响应标签时识别感兴趣行。理论部分给出存在性定理，证明在分数分离与集中条件下单头/多头模型以高概率恢复潜在行，并得到预测风险界。

Result: 模拟实验在回归与误设分类任务中，在不同样本量、维度和信号基数下均表现出强预测与定位能力；真实情感分析中提升了分类精度并获得可解释的 token 选择。

Conclusion: 注意力评分机制能够在异质矩阵值数据中定位样本特有信号，所提方法兼具理论保证与实证优势，为注意力机制提供了一种统计解释。

Abstract: Modern text and image representations are often matrix-valued, with rows corresponding to tokens, patches, or other local feature vectors. Predictive information is often sparse but sample-specific, making classical sparse regression methods with a common support poorly suited to this heterogeneity. This paper formalizes an individualized sparse regression framework for matrix-valued covariates in which each observation has its own rows of interest, while the associated regression effects are shared across the population. To estimate this model, we introduce a diagonalized attention mechanism that uses query--key scores to localize sample-specific signal rows and a value matrix for downstream regression. The proposed method has a parameter dimension independent of sample size and can identify rows of interest for new observations without their responses. We establish existence theorems showing that, under suitable score-separation and concentration conditions, single-head and multi-head diagonalized attention models recover the latent rows with high probability, yielding prediction risk bounds. Our theory therefore provides a statistical explanation of how attention-based scoring localizes sample-specific signals in heterogeneous matrix-valued data. Simulations demonstrate strong prediction and localization in regression and misspecified classification across varying sample sizes, dimensions, and signal cardinalities. Real sentiment analyses show improved classification accuracy and interpretable token selection.

</details>


### [146] [Sparse Identification for Automatic Large-Scale Screening: A Constraint-Aware Framework with Ultra Fast Decoding Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21321)
*Jianing Li,Li Chai,Yingcheng Lai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Logic Screening (LoSc) 方法，一种超快速、高精度且具备理论保证的大规模传染病筛查框架，仅需 O(k log n) 次混合检测即可准确识别所有阳性个体，并能显式处理样本用量与稀释效应等硬约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情初期需要在有限试剂和检测能力下，通过大规模筛查从大量样本中识别出极少数感染者；然而现有的组检测方法要么计算复杂度高，要么识别精度低，且缺少在样本用量约束与稀释效应等实际硬约束下进行稀疏识别的严格理论分析。

Method: LoSc 提出一种新颖的解码算法，采用极为简单的选择策略，并配合逻辑运算完成阳性解码，从而便于硬件直接实现与超快计算。其混合检测（pooling）设计显式纳入稀释效应与样本用量约束，并给出理论保证以指导最优的混合配置。

Result: LoSc 仅用 O(k log n) 次混合检测即可识别全部阳性个体，解码仅依赖逻辑运算、实现超快计算。大量仿真实验验证了该方法在有效性、效率和可扩展性方面的显著优势，并证明其优于现有组检测方法。

Conclusion: LoSc 为自动大规模筛查提供了一种快速、可靠且具有理论基础的解决方案，兼具高精度、超快计算与强可扩展性，适合疫情早期在有限资源条件下的实际部署。

Abstract: In the early stages of a pandemic, identification of a small number of infected individuals through large-scale screening is critical for pandemic control, yet remains challenging under limited reagents and testing capacity. Existing group testing methods suffer from either high computational complexity or low identification accuracy. Even worse, no available methods provide theoretically rigorous analysis for sparse identification with hard constraints caused by the sample usage constraint and the dilution effect existing ubiquitously in practical applications. In this article, we propose the Logic Screening method (LoSc), an ultra fast, accurate, and theoretically grounded framework for large-scale screening. LoSc introduces a novel decoding algorithm with a very simple selection strategy, achieving identification of all positives with only O(klogn) pooled tests. The decoding relies only on logical operations, enabling direct hardware implementation and yielding ultra fast computational implementation. Moreover, LoSc explicitly incorporates dilution and sample usage constraints into pooling designs, and establishes theoretical guarantees to guide optimal pooling configurations. Extensive simulations confirm the superior effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability. We believe LoSc offers a fast and reliable solution for automatic large-scale screening.

</details>


### [147] [Brownian Heads for Deep ReLU Representations: Activation Mass and the Cost of Same-Sample Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21422)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari,Nicole Mücke*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究深度ReLU表示后接有界范数预测器（Brownian头，即在加性或Lévy-Brownian RKHS中）的同样本选择问题，推导条件经验Rademacher复杂度的精确对偶恒等式与激活质量尺度下的锐界，并用层蛋糕/高斯投影恒等式将表示上确界化简为坐标级或符号投影阈值迹，从而分离已实现尺度与选择复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统深度表示学习常在同一批样本上选择隐藏特征并拟合最终预测器，事后（固定特征）分析会遗漏特征选择带来的代价；现有理论未能精确刻画同样本选择下表示上确界引入的额外复杂度，尤其是ReLU网络与RKHS预测器结合的缩放律。

Method: 研究对象为ReLU表示后接加性或Lévy-Brownian RKHS中的有界范数预测器（Brownian头）。对固定表示，利用对偶论证导出精确恒等式与以“激活质量”（观测隐藏向量平均范数）表达的锐界；对同样本选择，将表示上确界转化为二次Rademacher过程，借助Brownian层蛋糕与高斯投影恒等式化简为坐标级或符号投影阈值迹，分离已实现尺度与选择复杂度；对逐对互异输入，构造显式标量ReLU族以匹配有限迹与VC速率（在已实现迹-包络层面达到普适常数）；最后通过诱导范数收缩给出矩形、秩亏ReLU网络的架构级界，并以实验验证。

Result: 固定表示情形得到精确对偶恒等式和按激活质量的锐界；同样本选择情形得到分离尺度与复杂度项的界；显式ReLU族在已实现迹-包络层面匹配有限迹与VC速率至普适常数；诱导范数收缩给出矩形、秩亏网络的架构级界；实验验证了锐界与速率，展示了固定激活质量下的选择间隙（selection gap），并评估了Brownian头的预测可行性。

Conclusion: 同样本选择导致的表示上确界可被刻画为二次Rademacher过程，并通过Brownian恒等式分解为已实现尺度与选择复杂度之和；在适度假设下所得界在普适常数意义下是最优的，并可用于控制矩形与秩亏ReLU网络的复杂度，实验证实理论预测的选择间隙。

Abstract: Deep representation learning often selects hidden features and fits the final predictor on the same sample, so fixed-feature analysis performed after selection can omit selection cost. We study the conditional empirical Rademacher complexity of deep ReLU representations followed by bounded-norm predictors in additive or Lévy-Brownian RKHSs, termed Brownian heads. For a fixed representation, we derive an exact dual identity and sharp bounds in terms of activation mass, the average norm of the observed hidden vectors. Under same-sample selection, the representation supremum induces a quadratic Rademacher process. Brownian layer-cake and Gaussian-projection identities reduce it to coordinatewise or signed projected threshold traces, separating realized scale from selection complexity. For samples with pairwise-distinct inputs, explicit scalar ReLU families match the finite-trace and VC rates up to universal constants at the realized trace-and-envelope level. Induced-norm contraction also yields architecture-level bounds for rectangular, rank-deficient ReLU networks. Experiments verify the sharp bounds and rates, exhibit a selection gap at fixed activation mass, and assess the predictive feasibility of Brownian heads.

</details>


### [148] [Improving the Predictive Performance of Bootstrap Aggregating by Dirichlet Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21454)
*Quoc Viet Le,Joonha Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文基于Breiman"降低树间相关性而不削弱单棵树可提升随机森林"的观察，提出两种通过浓度参数α调控样本重加权的随机森林变体（DM与DW），给出判定其与标准随机森林无差异的简单理论准则用于轻量调参，并在公开分类基准上验证其持续具备竞争力且常优于其他RF基线，额外运行开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视Breiman的核心观察——在不削弱单棵树强度的前提下降低树间相关性能够改进随机森林——并据此探索通过受控的样本重加权来解耦并调控树间相关性与单个基学习器强度之间的关系，从而在不显著增加计算成本的情况下提升随机森林的泛化性能。

Method: 提出两种随机森林变体：Dirichlet-Multinomial Bagging随机森林（DM）和Dirichlet加权随机森林（DW）。二者均通过浓度参数 α>0 调节样本重加权机制（分别对应多项式bagging的Dirichlet先验与基于Dirichlet权重的加权方案）。同时给出一个简单的理论准则，用于刻画在何种条件下这些变体与标准随机森林在行为上不可区分，并利用该准则指导一种轻量级的调参策略。

Result: 在公开分类基准上的受控评估表明，DM与DW始终具有竞争力，并且在多种情形下优于其他随机森林基线（如标准随机森林及其他RF变体），同时带来的额外运行时间可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过Dirichlet分布调控样本重加权可以有效降低树间相关性、进而改进随机森林性能；所提出的理论准则为调参提供了简单依据，DM与DW在保持低计算开销的同时，在分类任务上取得了具有竞争力甚至更优的结果。

Abstract: We revisit Breiman's observation that reducing inter-tree correlation without weakening individual trees can improve random forests. Building on this principle, we introduce two variants: Dirichlet-Multinomial Bagging Random Forest (DM) and Dirichlet-Weighted Random Forest (DW). Both modulate sample reweighting via a concentration parameter $α>0$. We provide a simple theoretical criterion that clarifies when these variants behave indistinguishably from standard random forests, and we use it to guide a lightweight tuning strategy. In a controlled evaluation on public classification benchmarks, DM and DW are consistently competitive and often stronger than other random-forest (RF) baselines, with negligible additional runtime.

</details>


### [149] [How Many Posterior Samples? Calibrated Stopping for Adaptive Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21813)
*Vincent Corlay,Andriy Enttsel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究分类导向自适应感知中的停止层：如何将后验样本的投票转变为可靠的类别声明。作者指出"票数超过阈值即声明"的插件规则并不提供置信度保证，并提出校准后的固定样本规则、有限时域序贯规则以及精确缩减（curtailment）方法；进一步推导了单轮声明概率如何决定后验样本成本与分类精度，并在MNIST+DDRM上验证了显著的成本节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分类导向的自适应感知中，后验样本既引导下一步感知方向，其类标签又为候选类别投票并决定是否停止感知。现有做法常采用"观测票数份额超过阈值即声明"的插件式规则，但该阈值本身并非置信度保证。作者希望在不改动后验采样器和感知方向的前提下，设计能够以预设误声明概率（false-declaration probability）停止的停止层，并理解声明概率与成本、精度的关系。

Method: 作者聚焦于停止层。首先分析插件规则在投票质量恰等于阈值时的行为（约一半时间声明），说明其缺乏置信度保证。随后提出替代方案：(1) 校准一个固定样本规则，(2) 校准一个有限时域序贯规则到预设误声明概率，以及 (3) 精确缩减（exact curtailment）——对一个固定池规则，一旦其最终判定已被迫确定就提前停止。最后推导单轮声明概率如何决定沿感知路径的后验样本成本与分类精度。实验采用MNIST、DDRM以及固定的PCA引导探测序列。

Result: 在MNIST上使用DDRM和固定PCA引导探测序列，缩减方法最多节省62%的后验样本。在匹配的工作点（matched operating points）下比较各规则时，序贯停止规则节省成本最多；在较高精度下，同一条序贯规则可以用更多后验样本来换取更少的测量次数。

Conclusion: 插件式票数阈值规则不构成置信度保证，而经过校准的固定样本规则、有限时域序贯规则以及精确缩减方法能提供受控的误声明概率。缩减方案可显著降低后验样本成本，序贯停止在匹配工作点下成本节省最大，并能在高精度场景下实现"以后验样本换测量次数"的权衡。

Abstract: In classification-oriented adaptive sensing, posterior samples characterize uncertainty at the current measurement state and can serve two roles: they may guide the next sensing direction, while their class labels provide votes for the candidate classes and determine whether sensing should continue. We focus on the stopping layer that turns these votes into a declaration, without modifying the posterior sampler or sensing directions. A natural plug-in rule declares when the observed vote share exceeds a threshold. We show that this threshold is not itself a confidence guarantee: when the underlying vote mass equals the threshold, the plug-in rule declares about half the time. As alternatives, we calibrate a fixed-sample rule and a finite-horizon sequential rule to a prescribed false-declaration probability, and study exact curtailment, which stops a fixed-pool rule once its final verdict is forced. We then derive how one-round declaration probabilities determine posterior-sample cost and classification accuracy along a sensing path. On MNIST with DDRM and a fixed PCA-guided probe sequence, curtailment saves up to 62% of posterior samples. Among the evaluated rules at matched operating points, sequential stopping reduces the cost the most. At a high accuracy, that same sequential rule can trade more posterior samples for fewer measurements.

</details>


### [150] [Riemannian Simultaneous Inference for Tangent Vector Field Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21910)
*Xiaotian Chang,Yangdi Jiang,Qirui Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究黎曼流形（无边界）上非参数切向量场回归。由于不同点处的响应位于不同切空间，所提核估计量先将邻近响应平行移动到目标切空间，再作体积校正的局部平均。作者推导了其二阶一致偏差、有限带宽协方差与随机收敛速率；通过将切范数表示为单位切丛上的上确界，并对协方差精确白化，得到相关长度沿底流形为 h、沿纤维为 1 的单位方差高斯场，进而基于局部协方差几何导出带显式内在常数的 Gumbel 极限。结合高斯近似与交叉拟合协方差估计，构造了可行的同时置信管，并讨论了带宽选择与高阶偏差校正，模拟与全球风场数据重构验证了方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切向量场回归中，响应取值于流形上各点不同的切空间，无法直接比较或平均；同时需要为整个回归场提供统一的同时推断工具，刻画空间变化的不确定性。

Method: 提出非参数核估计量：先将邻近响应经平行移动拉回目标切空间，再做体积校正的局部平均。为构造同时置信管，将切范数写为单位切丛上的上确界，对协方差精确白化得到相关结构为（底流形尺度 h、纤维尺度 1）的单位方差高斯场，由其局部协方差几何得到 Gumbel 极限；再用高斯近似与交叉拟合协方差估计得到可行统计量，并辅以带宽选择与高阶偏差校正。

Result: 推导出估计量的二阶一致偏差、有限带宽协方差及随机收敛速率；建立显式内在常数的 Gumbel 极限；构造可行的同时置信管。模拟表明推断方法有效，全球风数据的随机重构展示了置信管横截面如何刻画随空间变化的误差不确定性。

Conclusion: 所提框架在黎曼流形上为切向量场回归提供了严格的同时推断工具，置信管能直观刻画空间变化的不确定性，且有理论与数值支持，并可通过带宽选择和高阶偏差校正改进有限样本表现。

Abstract: We consider nonparametric tangent vector field regression on a Riemannian manifold without boundary. Because responses at different points lie in different tangent spaces, the proposed kernel estimator first parallel transports nearby responses to the target tangent space and then forms a volume-corrected local average. We first derive its uniform second-order bias, finite-bandwidth covariance, and stochastic rate. For simultaneous inference, the tangent norm is written as a supremum over the unit tangent bundle. Exact covariance whitening gives a unit-variance Gaussian field whose correlation length is of order $h$ along the base manifold and of order one along the fibre. Its local covariance geometry leads to a Gumbel limit with an explicit intrinsic constant. Combining this limit with Gaussian approximation and cross-fitted covariance estimation yields a feasible simultaneous confidence tube for the regression field. We further discuss improved finite-sample inference with bandwidth selection and high-order bias corrections. Simulations on various manifolds support the proposed inference procedure. A randomized reconstruction of global wind data illustrates how the tube's cross-sections describe spatially varying uncertainty.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式AI分布学习在高维下样本复杂度呈 O(n^{-1/Θ(d)}) 的维数灾难问题，提出用“稀疏先验”刻画真实分布的结构，并定义“稀疏维度”k，证明在 k-稀疏先验下贝叶斯风险下界为 Ω(√(k/n))，且TV距离下存在（渐近对数因子意义上）匹配的上界，从而以对 n 的依赖克服了维数灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI分布学习的理论保证在 d 维下随 n 的退化速度为 O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})，虽已被证明是极小极大最优，但作者认为这过于悲观：仅靠光滑性假设不足以刻画真实应用中分布常见的结构，因此需要引入能捕捉分布稀疏结构的先验，以改善样本复杂度的理论界。

Method: 作者引入“稀疏先验”这一分布类，并在所有分布空间上定义“稀疏维度”（Sparse Dimension）k 作为先验稀疏性的度量。在 k-稀疏先验下，对常见距离度量证明分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为 Ω(√(k/n))；在温和附加假设下，对总变差（TV）距离给出（在 n,k 渐近意义下相差对数因子）匹配的上界。同时证明了贝叶斯设定下分布学习与“学会采样”的统计等价性，从而将结果推广到学习采样任务。

Result: 在 k-稀疏先验下，分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为 Ω(√(k/n))，且对 TV 距离存在与之匹配（渐近至对数因子）的上界；分布学习与学习采样在贝叶斯意义下统计等价。尽管 k 仍可能依赖于维度 d 或某种内在维数，但结果表明，在合适的先验下，学习过程克服了对样本量 n 依赖的维数灾难。

Conclusion: 仅假定光滑性导致现有学习界过于悲观，而引入反映分布结构的稀疏先验后，能以 √(k/n) 的速率学习，突破对 n 的维数灾难。该结果在贝叶斯框架下同时适用于分布学习与学习采样，为生成式AI的理论保证提供了更贴合实际结构的分析路径。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [152] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BI-Bench，首个系统评估LLM端到端BI能力的基准，并设计工具增强的BI-Agent及后训练框架，显著提升BI问题回答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BI工作流要求用户手动完成识别相关表、数据转换、建立连接关系等繁琐步骤后才能回答业务问题，过程复杂耗时。鉴于LLM在处理数据方面的强大能力，作者希望研究其能否端到端回答BI问题，从而免除用户的手动准备工作。

Method: 作者从公开来源收集大量真实BI项目，并从真实用户仪表盘中人工提取（问题，标准答案）对，构建BI-Bench基准；设计工具增强的BI-Agent，将BI工作流分解为搜索、连接、转换等结构化数据子任务，并在各BI阶段编排专门的数据管理方法；同时开发后训练框架，从真实BI项目合成训练轨迹，结合监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）对BI-Agent进行后训练。

Result: 前沿LLM在BI-Bench上准确率不足50%；BI-Agent相比基础LLM带来最高40个百分点的准确率提升；经后训练的BI-Agent进一步获得最高30个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 在复杂BI工作流中，将工具增强推理与领域特定后训练相结合至关重要。该研究为LLM端到端处理BI任务指出了有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [153] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ETA 是一种端到端可训练的稀疏注意力架构，通过预测动态上下文阈值实现近似稠密模型质量的同时，带来最高 2.5 倍的长上下文解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，庞大的 KV 缓存导致严重的内存带宽瓶颈；现有稀疏注意力方法虽通过选择性加载缓解该问题，但依赖僵化的启发式规则会丢弃必要上下文，造成质量下降。

Method: 提出 Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)：直接从 query 表征预测动态上下文阈值，对困难检索/推理步骤分配类稠密上下文，对常规 token 进行剪枝。训练时采用乘法抑制将低于阈值的 logit 向零压缩（而非直接删除），形成平滑的均匀注意力底，既避免表征塌缩，又消除初始 token 上的局部注意力汇点；推理时据此硬剪枝无信息 KV 块。此外，实现基于 Triton 的定制解码内核，利用缓存的几何-概率界以 O(1) 时间筛选 KV 块，并引入离线校准算法冻结每头常数阈值以消除预测器开销。

Result: 1.45B 预训练 ETA 模型在语言建模、常识推理和长上下文检索任务上媲美稠密注意力，训练稀疏度约 85%、有效解码密度约 38%；在长达 512K token 的序列上，定制 Triton 内核相对 FlashAttention-2 实现最高 2.5 倍墙钟解码加速；离线校准冻结阈值后，注意力计算额外减少 27%。

Conclusion: ETA 证明了通过可学习的动态阈值可以在不牺牲稠密模型质量的前提下实现硬件友好的长上下文加速，为稀疏注意力提供了兼具质量与速度的实用方案，并可通过离线校准进一步降低部署开销。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [154] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bio-MF 是一种免隐变量、一步式 MeanFlow 框架，用于以 EEG 为条件生成 fNIRS 信号；它通过信号空间 x-预测、MeanFlow 速度监督和单次网络评估实现低延迟合成，并借助时空交互 4D 编码、跨模态无分类器引导和噪声门控 FFT 正则化保持任务相关的血流动力学结构，在提升混合 MI 解码精度的同时实现约 857 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合 EEG-fNIRS 的运动想象脑机接口能优于单一 EEG 系统，但配对采集常不可用或不方便；现有 EEG-to-fNIRS 跨模态生成方法生成缓慢且常需预训练，难以用于实时 MI-BCI。而一步式生成模型虽可降低延迟，却可能降低保真度并引入非生理性伪影。因此需要一种既能快速生成又能保持任务相关生成质量的方案。

Method: 提出 Bio-MF：一种免隐变量的单步 MeanFlow 框架。核心设计包括：(1) 直接在信号空间进行 x-预测，将信号空间输出转换为 MeanFlow 速度监督，并以单次网络评估完成推理；(2) 集成时空交互 4D 编码（Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding）以在异构传感器布局下保持任务相关的血流动力学结构；(3) 采用跨模态无分类器引导（cross-modal classifier-free guidance）与噪声电平门控的 FFT 正则化（noise-level-gated FFT regularization）以抑制伪影、提升保真度。在两个数据集上评估：Dataset 1 使用原始电极布局，Dataset 2 使用未见过的 64 通道 EEG 蒙太奇以验证泛化性。

Result: 在 Dataset 1 上，EEG + 合成 fNIRS 相比仅 EEG 的 ACC 提升 HbR 为 3.37 个百分点、HbO 为 4.15 个百分点；在 Dataset 2（未见过的 64 通道 EEG 蒙太奇）上，对应增益仍为 2.98 和 2.50 个百分点。在 RTX PRO 6000 GPU 上，Bio-MF 生成单个 fNIRS 试次仅需 7.0 ms，相较于 1000 步 SCDM 延迟实现 857 倍加速。

Conclusion: Bio-MF 能够以极低延迟完成 EEG 到 fNIRS 的合成，同时保持下游混合 MI 解码所需的任务相关生成质量，在实时 MI-BCI 场景中兼具速度与有效性，并提供了开源代码。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/psychosiwa/Bio-MF.

</details>


### [155] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用神经网络参数化的随机微分方程（SDE）对类星体光变曲线建模的历史，并提出一种依赖过去状态的连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降（Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD），在二维景观上相较普通SGD表现出更广探索和更精确收敛；此外还提出一种无需求解HJB偏微分方程的连续时间策略梯度强化学习结构，其最优性条件可恢复以往工作中的Gibbs策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类星体亮度呈随机变化，其光变曲线中蕴含超大质量黑洞的信息，从地基巡天时间序列中建模这种随机变化是统计难题。为克服该难题，作者回顾了用神经网络参数化SDE的历史，并进一步探索将延迟记忆与（连续时间）随机过程思想推广到优化算法（SGD）和强化学习策略中。

Method: 作者提出Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent（连续延迟记忆SGD），使迭代过程依赖于过去状态；在二维景观上进行模拟，并通过调节超参数与Vanilla SGD对比探索范围与收敛精度。此外，提出一种连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构，用于探索性策略，且无需求解HJB偏微分方程。

Result: 在二维景观模拟中，通过调整超参数，Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD相较Vanilla SGD表现出更宽的探索范围和更精确的收敛行为。所提强化学习结构的最优性条件可恢复此前研究中的Gibbs策略。

Conclusion: 神经网络参数化的SDE为类星体光变曲线等随机过程建模提供了有效途径；将延迟记忆引入随机梯度下降能改善探索与收敛性能；连续时间策略梯度方法能绕开HJB PDE求解并恢复Gibbs策略，为相关工作提供了新视角。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>


### [156] [Do Quantum Models Scale Like LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20912)
*David S. Berman,Ying-Jer Kao,Roger G. Melko,Alexander G. Stapleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 RydbergGPT（一种在里德堡原子阵列量子测量数据上训练的自回归 Transformer）的神经缩放定律，发现临界点附近的损失随数据量呈幂律（带损失下限修正），并借用量子/语言两种数据的两点互信息函数，论证多尺度依赖有利于稳定神经缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经缩放定律是否源于数据本身的多尺度相关结构。作者利用 Rydberg 量子系统在激光失谐参数调节下表现出临界点有限尺寸残留这一特性，作为可调谐的多尺度依赖数据源，将其与自然语言语料进行比较，以检验“缩放行为是模型-数据配对的性质”这一假设。

Method: 训练自回归 Transformer 模型 RydbergGPT，输入为里德堡原子阵列的量子比特投影测量数据；考察模型损失随训练集大小的变化，并用带损失下限修正的幂律拟合。同时定义一种熵归一化、有限样本修正的互信息“两点”函数，用以比较 Rydberg 测量数据与自然语言语料的统计结构。

Result: 在临界点附近，Transformer 损失随训练数据量的关系可用带损失下限的幂律很好地描述；远离临界点时幂律拟合质量显著下降。两点函数分析显示，近临界统计的两点函数与自然语言最为接近，而远离临界点的比特构型其两点函数衰减更快。

Conclusion: 结果支持多尺度依赖有助于稳定神经缩放这一假设，并说明缩放行为应被视为模型-数据配对的一种性质，而非仅由模型或数据单方面决定。

Abstract: In this work, we study the neural scaling laws of RydbergGPT, an autoregressive transformer model trained on qubit projective measurement data gathered from interacting Rydberg atom arrays. The quantum system is known to exhibit a finite-size remnant of a critical point as the laser detuning parameter is varied. We find that near the critical point the transformer loss as a function of training dataset size is well described by a power-law with a loss floor correction. However, away from criticality the quality of the power-law description is substantially reduced. We then compare the statistical structure of both Rydberg measurements and natural-language corpora using an entropy-normalised, finite sample corrected mutual information "two-point" function. We find that near-critical statistics of the two point functions are closest to those observed in natural-language, whilst other qubit configurations far from the critical point have two-point functions that decay more rapidly. This supports the hypothesis that multi-scale dependence contributes to stable neural scaling, and that scaling behaviour should be viewed as a property of the model-data pair.

</details>


### [157] [When AI Reviews Train AI Reviewers: Scientific-Judgment Collapse and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20942)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究AI审稿的递归反馈风险：用模型生成评论训练后继模型会导致评分分布收窄、语义多样性下降（作者称之为"科学判断坍缩"），并提出TrustReviewer系统，通过训练期数据精筛与测试期激活引导来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地参与科学评审，其生成评论会进入公开数据与未来训练语料，形成"递归式AI评审"：后一代审稿模型学习前代模型的判断。作者担忧这种反馈循环会逐步侵蚀评审的多样性与客观性，因此希望在一个受控环境中量化其一阶风险。

Method: 作者以Llama 3.1 8B为基座，先用2018—2023年ICLR官方评审微调出一个审稿模型，再在ICLR 2024数据上训练四个后继模型，训练数据中人评与模型生成评论按不同比例混合。通过测量评分分布与同论文/语料层级的语义多样性来观察坍缩现象。随后提出TrustReviewer：训练期采用单阶段训练，使用经过策展的语料以剔除低质量与语义退化的监督信号；测试期采用成对激活引导（paired activation steering），无需额外训练或专家标注即可校正残余的坍缩倾向。

Result: 研究发现，引入合成评论会压缩评分分布，并降低同一论文内及语料层面的语义多样性，即出现"科学判断坍缩"（scientific-judgment collapse）。TrustReviewer通过训练期预防与测试期校正两阶段干预，有效缓解了坍缩倾向，并在提升推荐对齐度（recommendation alignment）、保持评审多样性方面相对基线有所改善。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了递归式审稿模型训练的一个具体风险——科学判断坍缩，并提供了实用的缓解手段（TrustReviewer），有望在AI辅助科学评审中保持判断多样性并改善推荐对齐，为构建更稳健的AI评审系统提供了思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, both as automated reviewers and as assistants to human reviewers. As model-generated reviews enter public data and future training corpora, AI peer review can become recursive: later reviewers learn from judgments produced by earlier models. We study one step of this feedback loop in a controlled setting. Starting from Llama 3.1 8B, we first fine-tune a reviewer on official ICLR reviews from 2018--2023 and then train four successor models on ICLR 2024 data with systematically varied mixtures of official and model-generated reviews. Our study shows that introducing synthetic reviews compresses rating distributions and reduces both same-paper and corpus-level semantic diversity. We call this pattern $\textbf{scientific-judgment collapse}$.
  To mitigate this failure mode, we introduce $\textbf{TrustReviewer}$, an open-source LLM-based system for generating peer reviews of AI and machine learning papers. TrustReviewer intervenes at two complementary stages. For training-time prevention, we train the core reviewer in a single stage on a curated corpus designed to reduce low-quality and semantically degenerate supervision. For test-time correction, paired activation steering aims to further mitigate residual tendencies toward collapsed judgments without further training or additional expert annotation. Together, these results characterize a concrete risk of recursive reviewer training and provide practical interventions for preserving judgment diversity and improving recommendation alignment in AI-assisted scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [158] [Efficient Bayes-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20954)
*Jonathan Hau,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端的基于模型的强化学习算法，将LTL任务（表示为LDBA）与环境的贝叶斯自适应MDP（BAMDP）同步，通过贝叶斯探索-利用权衡与新颖的BAMCP规划算法，在未知环境中高效合成满足线性时序逻辑规范的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于LTL的强化学习方法多依赖非贝叶斯、无模型框架，在未知环境中样本效率低、探索-利用权衡不足，难以高效满足安全、可达性等时序逻辑任务。

Method: 将LTL规范转化为限界确定型Büchi自动机（LDBA），并与环境的贝叶斯自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）同步，形成同步的BAMDP结构；在此结构上提出新颖的贝叶斯自适应蒙特卡洛规划（BAMCP）算法，实现近似贝叶斯最优策略合成。

Result: 在有限与无限时域任务实验中，该方法在性质满足率和样本效率上均优于传统无模型方法；消融实验表明提出的新BAMCP算法相比经典BAMCP更能满足LTL任务；同时在谨慎强化学习场景下成功减少训练过程中的任务违规次数。

Conclusion: 该端到端基于模型的方法通过贝叶斯自适应框架与改进的BAMCP规划，能够在未知环境中更高效、更安全地合成满足LTL规范的策略，并显著提升样本效率与降低任务违规。

Abstract: We present a novel end-to-end model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm for efficient policy synthesis under given Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specifications (e.g., safety or reachability) in unknown environments. To do so, a Limit-Deterministic B{ü}chi Automaton (LDBA) representation of the LTL task is synchronised with a Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Process (BAMDP) representation of the environment, which allows us to leverage an enhanced exploration-exploitation trade-off that is achieved via Bayesian RL, as opposed to traditional non-Bayesian approaches. We further propose a novel Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning (BAMCP) algorithm to allow for approximate Bayes-optimal strategy synthesis in the synchronised BAMDP construct. A range of finite- and infinite-horizon task experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of both property satisfaction and sample efficiency, when compared to traditional model-free approaches. Additional ablation studies also successfully highlight the value of the novel BAMCP algorithm in comparison to classical BAMCP for LTL task satisfaction. Finally, we also showcase a successful application of our approach for \textit{cautious} RL, namely to reduce the number of task violations incurred during policy training.

</details>


### [159] [From Switching to Dynamic Regret: A Simple Reduction via Unbiased Random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20968)
*Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简单框架，将非平稳在线学习中的动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化，从而可利用现成的带切换遗憾保证的算法直接导出动态遗憾界，且对强凸、指数凹和一般凸损失均达到极小极大最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳在线学习中，动态遗憾作为衡量学习器相对时变比较序列表现的重要指标日益受到关注。尽管已有大量进展，但针对强凸和指数凹损失获得最优动态遗憾界往往需要复杂精细的分析。作者希望提供一个统一且简洁的归约框架，避免针对不同损失逐一进行繁琐推导。

Method: 核心思路是将动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化：对任意比较者序列构造一个逐轮无偏的辅助随机序列，并控制其方差与切换次数；再结合合适的代理（surrogate）损失，把动态遗憾分解为对该随机序列的期望切换遗憾与可控方差之和。由此可直接借助现成的切换遗憾算法得到动态遗憾界。

Result: 对强凸和指数凹损失，建立 $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ 的动态遗憾界（其中 $T$ 为时间范围，$P_T$ 为比较序列的路径长度）；对一般凸损失，同一归约恢复 $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ 的动态遗憾界。

Conclusion: 所提出的归约框架简单通用，三类损失（强凸、指数凹、一般凸）下的结果均匹配极小极大最优界，凸显了该框架的多功能性与有效性。

Abstract: In non-stationary online learning, dynamic regret has attracted increasing attention as a measure of how well an online learner performs against a time-varying comparator sequence. Despite considerable advances, attaining optimal bounds for strongly convex and exp-concave losses often involves intricate analysis. In this paper, we present a \textit{simple} framework that reduces dynamic regret minimization to switching regret minimization. As a result, we can derive dynamic regret bounds by using off-the-shelf algorithms with switching regret guarantees. The key idea of our reduction is to construct, for \textit{any} comparator sequence, an auxiliary random sequence that is unbiased at each round, with the controlled variance and a manageable number of switches. Combining this construction with suitable surrogate losses, we can decompose dynamic regret into the expected switching regret against the random sequence and its controlled variance. Theoretically, for strongly convex and exp-concave losses, we establish the $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ dynamic regret bounds, where $T$ denotes the time horizon and $P_T$ denotes the path-length of the comparator sequence. Moreover, for general convex losses, the same reduction also recovers the $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ dynamic regret bound. Notably, all our findings match the minimax optimal results for these three types of losses, highlighting the versatility of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [160] [Generative inversion for early ranking of competing geologic interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20978)
*Harun Ur Rashid,Daniel O'Malley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种工作流，将相互竞争的地质解释转化为替代性空间先验，并依据其与地下水位（水头）观测数据的一致性进行排序，从而在数据稀缺的早期阶段甄别最现实的地质模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高后果地下工程决策常在严重数据稀缺下做出，专家对同一地下系统可能给出相互竞争的解释，但在项目早期缺乏实用手段判断哪种解释最符合实际，且这种不确定性往往要等钻多口井（耗资数百万美元）后才能消除。现有方法要么依赖主观判断，要么需要早期阶段难以获得的密集数据。

Method: 提出一种工作流：对每种地质解释，用文本到图像基础模型生成1600张地质图像集合；用单独训练的变分自编码器（VAE）提供解释特定的潜表示；监督式逆网络将水头观测映射到潜空间；冻结的解码器生成图像并映射为对数导水率场；稳态流动模拟给出预测水头；将模拟与观测的失配转换为高斯形式的兼容性分数，据此对解释排序。框架在基于Johansen地层的合成基准（含三种一致性递减的解释）上评估，并应用于WIPP（废物隔离试验厂）Culebra白云岩段两个已发表概念模型。

Result: 在925个测试案例中，平均水头RMSE从"精确且准确"解释的0.197增至"准确"解释的0.227，再到"失配"解释的0.280。在WIPP Culebra案例中，修订模型获得0.991的兼容性权重，而原始模型仅0.009，与独立证据一致。

Conclusion: 该工作流能够利用有限的水头观测数据客观、定量地评估竞争性地质解释的现实性，在数据稀缺的早期阶段即可有效排序，其结论与独立证据吻合，为高后果地下决策提供了实用工具。

Abstract: High-consequence subsurface decisions are often made under severe data scarcity. Experts may arrive at competing interpretations of the same subsurface system, yet early in a project there is rarely a practical way to determine which one is most realistic. This uncertainty can persist until several wells are drilled, often costing millions of dollars. Existing approaches for evaluating geologic interpretations rely either on subjective judgment or on dense data that are rarely available in early-stage investigations. We present a workflow that addresses this challenge by translating competing geologic interpretations into alternative spatial priors and ranking them according to their consistency with hydraulic-head observations. For each interpretation, a text-to-image foundation model generates an ensemble of 1600 geologic images, and a separately trained variational autoencoder provides an interpretation-specific latent representation. A supervised inverse network maps the head observations into this latent space, and the frozen decoder produces an image that is mapped to a log-conductivity field. Steady-state flow simulation then provides predicted heads, and the resulting mismatch is converted into a Gaussian-form compatibility score. We evaluate the framework using a synthetic benchmark based on the Johansen Formation and three interpretations of decreasing consistency with the reference representation. Across 925 test cases, the mean head RMSE increases from 0.197 for the Precise \& Accurate interpretation to 0.227 for the Accurate interpretation and 0.280 for the Mismatched interpretation. We subsequently apply the workflow to two published conceptual models of the Culebra Dolomite Member at the Waste Isolation Pilot Plant. The revised model receives a compatibility weight of 0.991, compared with 0.009 for the original model, consistent with the independent evidence.

</details>


### [161] [ASGARD: Action-Space Guard for UAV Resilience via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20982)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASGARD 提出一种两阶段教师-学生流水线，通过动作攻击感知的潜在表示和运行时纠正监视器，使基于强化学习的无人机控制器在动作空间攻击下仍能完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于强化学习的无人机导航控制器容易受到动作空间攻击（在策略生成动作之后、执行器执行之前篡改动作指令）。现有防御多聚焦于策略输入的对抗攻击，而针对动作空间攻击的方案往往需要在训练时重训策略，无法在运行时应对被破坏的动作，因而缺乏对运行时动作攻击的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 ASGARD 两阶段教师-学生流水线：教师阶段，编码器将无人机物理状态与动作攻击相关的特权信息结合，生成动作攻击感知的潜在表示，用于训练 RL 控制策略和一个监视器，由监视器向执行器输出修正后的动作指令；学生阶段，编码器和监视器通过监督学习从教师对应部分蒸馏，仅依赖无人机物理状态历史即可在机载运行。

Result: ASGARD 在针对无人机不同动作指令的多种攻击场景下进行评估，结果表明其对动作空间攻击具有鲁棒性，能够在遭受攻击的情况下完成任务；此外，ASGARD 能够泛化到未见过的攻击，并对隐蔽攻击仍保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: ASGARD 通过教师-学生蒸馏设计，实现了无人机 RL 控制器对动作空间攻击的运行时韧性，兼具泛化性与对隐蔽攻击的鲁棒性，验证了该方案的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) controllers have been recently adopted for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) navigation and control. However, they are susceptible to action-space attacks that overwrite the action commands after the policy generates them and before the actuators execute them. While most existing defenses target attacks on the policy's inputs, those addressing action-space attacks retrain the policy at training time and are not resilient to corrupted actions at runtime. We propose ASGARD, a two-phase teacher-student pipeline for making RL-based UAV control resilient to action-space attacks. In the teacher phase, an encoder combines the UAV's physical state with action-attack-related privileged information to produce an action-attack-aware latent that trains the RL control policy and a monitor that outputs corrected action commands to the actuators. In the student phase, both the encoder and the monitor are trained via supervised learning from their teacher counterparts to run on-board using only the UAV's physical state history. We evaluate ASGARD across attack scenarios targeting different action commands on UAV. We find that ASGARD is resilient to action-space attacks and completes the missions despite the attack. We further find that ASGARD generalizes to unseen attacks and remains resilient against stealthy attacks.

</details>


### [162] [From Stress to Affect: Multimodal Deep Learning for Physiological Emotion Recognition Across Wearable Sensor Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20991)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对可穿戴生理情绪识别的时序深度学习架构进行了系统比较，在WESAD和EmoWear两个多模态数据集上评估了LSTM、TCN和Transformer模型，发现最优架构取决于数据集特性，多模态感知普遍优于单模态，且4Hz采样频率即可在低训练成本下获得与高频率相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理情绪识别研究通常只评估单一模型、单一传感器配置或单一数据集，缺乏对不同架构、感知模态和采样频率如何共同影响识别性能的系统性理解，难以指导实际应用中的模型与配置选择。

Method: 作者在WESAD和EmoWear两个多模态可穿戴数据集上，比较了双向LSTM、TCN和Transformer三种时序深度学习架构，在仅腕部、仅胸部及多模态三种传感器配置下，采用参与者独立的留一受试者交叉验证（LOSO-CV）进行评估，并进一步分析软投票集成、传感器消融、采样频率和基于梯度的显著性。

Result: Transformer在WESAD多模态任务上取得最高准确率（99.02% ± 0.51%）；LSTM在EmoWear的唤醒（91.80% ± 1.06%）和效价（89.96% ± 0.36%）上均获最佳多模态准确率。结果表明架构相对性能取决于数据集特性，而非某一种架构普遍占优；多模态感知在两个数据集上均一致优于单一腕部或胸部配置；采样频率分析显示4Hz为实用工作点，在显著降低训练成本的同时性能与更高频率相当。

Conclusion: 研究为可穿戴生理情绪识别中的架构选择、感知模态组合和采样频率设定提供了明确指导：应根据数据集特点选择最优架构，优先采用多模态感知配置，并以4Hz作为兼顾性能与成本的实用采样频率。

Abstract: Physiological emotion recognition using wearable sensors has important applications in mental health monitoring, affective computing, and human-computer interaction. However, existing studies typically evaluate a single model, sensing configuration, or dataset, limiting our understanding of how these factors influence recognition performance. We present a comparative study of temporal deep learning architectures for physiological emotion recognition using two multimodal wearable datasets: WESAD and EmoWear. Bidirectional long short-term memory (LSTM), temporal convolutional network (TCN), and Transformer models are evaluated under wrist-only, chest-only, and multimodal sensing configurations using participant-independent leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOSO-CV). We also investigate soft-voting ensembles, sensor ablation, sampling frequency, and gradient-based saliency. The Transformer achieved the highest multimodal accuracy on WESAD (99.02% +/- 0.51%), whereas the LSTM achieved the best multimodal accuracy on EmoWear for both arousal (91.80% +/- 1.06%) and valence (89.96% +/- 0.36%). These results show that relative architecture performance depends on dataset characteristics rather than one architecture being uniformly superior. Multimodal sensing consistently outperformed wrist-only and chest-only configurations across both datasets. Sampling-frequency analysis showed that 4 Hz provides a practical operating point, with performance comparable to higher frequencies at substantially lower training cost. These findings provide guidance for selecting architectures, sensing modalities, and sampling frequencies for wearable physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [163] [MOSAIC-SR: Transformer-Guided Symbolic Regression for Scientific Equation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20997)
*Peiyi Zheng,Yanming Kang,Hans De Sterck,Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MOSAIC-SR，一种结合预训练 Transformer 与搜索优化的符号回归方法：用神经网络快速生成多个初始表达式草图以定位有前景的搜索区域，再通过尺度感知常数优化与局部符号修复联合恢复结构与常数，在多个基准上取得最高的符号解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法难以兼顾灵活的结构搜索与高效推理：搜索类方法能精化结构但依赖代价高昂的组合优化和随机初始化；预训练神经模型能快速生成公式但常含符号错误。因此需要一个能结合两者优势的框架。

Method: MOSAIC-SR 使用预训练 Transformer 提出多个初始表达式草图，用这些草图在多个有前景区域初始化搜索，避免在巨大表达式空间中的随机起点。每次搜索通过尺度感知的常数优化（scale-aware constant optimization）与局部符号修复（local symbolic repair）联合恢复结构和常数。

Result: 在 SRSD-Feynman 数据集（含/不含哑变量）以及六个额外基准上评估，MOSAIC-SR 在所有数据集均取得最高的符号解率（symbolic solution rate），预测精度位列前两名；在存在无关哑输入的条件下该优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 学习得到的先验可以将搜索聚焦于有前景的方程结构，数值优化与符号修复对成功恢复方程至关重要。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to recover closed-form equations from observations, providing interpretable models for scientific discovery. Existing approaches struggle to combine flexible structural search with efficient inference. Search-based methods can refine expression structure but often rely on costly combinatorial optimization with random initialization. Pretrained neural models generate formulas almost instantly, but their predictions often contain symbolic errors. We introduce MOSAIC-SR, which uses a pretrained Transformer to propose multiple initial sketches. These sketches initialize searches in several promising regions, avoiding random starts in the vast expression space. Each search jointly recovers structure and constants through scale-aware constant optimization and local symbolic repair. We evaluate MOSAIC-SR on the SRSD-Feynman dataset with and without dummy variables and on six additional benchmarks. MOSAIC-SR obtains the highest symbolic solution rate on every dataset while ranking among the top two methods in predictive accuracy. This advantage persists in the presence of irrelevant dummy inputs. The results show that learned priors can focus search on promising equation structures, and that numerical optimization and symbolic repair are important for recovery.

</details>


### [164] [On the Limits of Maximal Coding Rate Reduction for Out-of-Distribution Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21001)
*Menghui Zhou,Gaoshan Bi,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究最大编码率降低（MCR²）目标在分布偏移（OOD）下的泛化局限，发现其可因依赖不稳定环境特征而在编码最优的同时完全预测失败，即使引入 IRM/REx 的不变性原理也无法消除该失败，因此需要对 MCR² 提出新的假设或学习原则才能获得可靠的 OOD 保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量工作致力于使深度学习的表示与架构可解释，MCR² 作为学习类别子流形结构化、判别性表示的信息论框架，启发了可解释的白盒架构，但作者观察到 MCR² 在分布偏移下可能彻底失效，因此需要系统研究其 OOD 泛化的理论极限。

Method: 作者通过构造可精确达到全局编码最优的反例，从理论上分析 MCR² 目标的 OOD 泛化能力。其一，构造仅依赖不稳定环境特征却能取得全局编码最优的表示，在相关性反转后完全预测失败，即使在存在完美稳定特征的情况下亦然；该精确最优示例包含训练中不可能出现的测试输入。其二，考察即使测试输入都可在训练中出现的情形，证明编码质量可任意逼近最优而预测误差任意逼近 100%。其三，将 IRM 与 REx 所依据的不变性原理直接纳入 MCR²，验证该失败表示在跨训练环境间仍满足相同的编码最优算子，从而说明共享编码最优性并不能保证稳定预测。

Result: 研究发现 MCR² 存在两项 OOD 泛化局限：(1) 仅靠 MCR² 目标即可允许完全预测失败——完全基于不稳定环境特征的表示能达到全局编码最优，却在相关性反转后彻底失败；(2) 直接引入 IRM/REx 的不变性原理不能消除该失败，失败的表示在各训练环境间享有同一最优编码算子，表明共享编码最优性无法确保稳定预测。因此，可靠的 OOD 保证需要额外的假设或新学习原则来建立跨环境的稳定预测关系。

Conclusion: MCR² 在分布偏移下的泛化保证存在根本性缺陷，现有不变性原理不足以修复这一缺陷；要为 MCR² 建立可靠的 OOD 保证，必须引入额外的新假设或学习原则，以建立跨环境的稳定预测关系。

Abstract: Substantial efforts have been devoted to making deep learning objectives, representations, and architectures interpretable, with the goal of improving the safety, robustness, and generalisation of learning systems in diverse real-world applications. The recently proposed maximal coding rate reduction ($\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$) offers a promising information-theoretic framework for learning structured, discriminative representations of class-wise submanifolds and has inspired interpretable white-box architectures. However, we observe that $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ can completely fail under distribution shift, motivating our study of its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation limits. We establish two limitations of $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ for OOD generalisation. First, the $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ objective alone can admit complete prediction failure: a representation based entirely on unstable environmental features can achieve the global coding optimum yet fail completely after correlation reversal, despite an available perfectly stable feature. This exact-optimum example includes test inputs that cannot occur during training. Even when every possible test input can also occur during training, coding quality can be arbitrarily close to optimal while prediction error is arbitrarily close to 100%. Second, directly incorporating the invariance principle underlying widely successful invariant risk minimisation (IRM) and risk extrapolation (REx) does not eliminate this failure. The failing representation admits the same optimal coding operator across training environments, showing that shared coding optimality does not ensure stable prediction. Reliable OOD guarantees for $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ therefore require additional new assumptions or learning principles that establish stable predictive relationships across environments.

</details>


### [165] [Scaling Discovery through Test-Time Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21032)
*Jongho Park,Vasilis Kontonis,Shivam Garg,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究测试时多智能体通信的效果，发现通信智能体团队在 ARC-AGI-3、多边形装箱、MNIST 分类器压缩等任务上显著优于独立并行智能体，且优势随规模增长，但需要充足算力和明确反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学进步依赖协作，但现有智能体系统很少体现这一点；关于通信智能体是否有用，此前结果矛盾，仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 作者研究无预定义角色、通过共享目录通信的多智能体测试时通信，设置 team@k（k 个通信智能体）与 4k 个独立智能体及 best@k 等基线进行对比，在多个基准上评估。

Result: 在 ARC-AGI-3 上，k 个通信智能体的成功率匹配 4k 个独立智能体，且优势随 k 增大而增长；团队可可靠解决任何单个智能体都无法解决的任务。在多边形装箱上通信智能体超越 best@k 及已知最佳分数；在 MNIST 压缩上，四个智能体团队产出 1957 字节、99.4% 准确率，优于已知最佳人类方案和最佳单智能体结果。但当算力受限或缺乏明确进展度量时，独立智能体可能更优。

Conclusion: 在充足算力和清晰反馈条件下，多智能体通信一致地带来更强结果；其收益并非无条件，但总体表明测试时通信是一种可扩展的有效协作机制。

Abstract: Science advances not in isolation but through collaboration, yet existing agentic systems capture little of this. Whether communicating agents help remains an open question with mixed prior results. We show that test-time communication can substantially outperform independent parallel attempts on challenging tasks, where sharing a breakthrough can push the whole group forward. We first study the effect of scaling multi-agent test-time communication, where agents have no predefined roles and communicate via a shared directory, on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark requiring novel problem solving. We find that a team of $k$ communicating agents, team@$k$, matches the success rate of $4k$ independent agents, and this advantage grows with $k$, suggesting gains compound with scale. The effect is not merely efficiency: a task that no single agent can solve, a team of agents can solve reliably. Furthermore, these gains transfer to research-oriented tasks, given sufficient compute. On polyomino packing, communicating agents outperform best@$k$ and exceed the prior best-known score. On MNIST classifier compression, communication surpasses the best-known human solution. A team of four agents produced a 1,957-byte classifier submission achieving 99.4% test accuracy, smaller than both the best-known human solution and the best single-agent result. These gains are not unconditional. Independent agents may outperform communication when compute is limited or when a clear measure of progress is absent. However, under sufficient compute and clear feedback, multi-agent communication consistently yields stronger results.

</details>


### [166] [Layerwise Decoupling for Stable Structured Sparsification of Fully Connected Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21126)
*Charles Kulick,Armenak Petrosyan,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦的逐层结构化稀疏方法，将预训练网络的全连接层分解为浅层双端子网络，对每个块的外层权重施加结构化组惩罚，逐层剪枝神经元以减小层宽，并证明该解耦目标在最优性上等价于特定的联合惩罚，且比耦合方法更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合惩罚（coupled）方法对所有层同时施加结构化稀疏约束，容易出现可用的正则强度范围较窄、过度剪枝（catastrophic over-pruning）等问题。作者旨在通过解耦、逐层的重构，提升结构化剪枝的鲁棒性，同时保持精度。

Method: 将每个全连接层分解为浅层两子网（内层+外层）；归一化内层权重；对外层权重矩阵施加结构化组惩罚；逐层顺序处理以剪除神经元并减小各层宽度。利用正齐次激活函数的性质，证明约束的解耦目标在最优性上等价于对内、外层权重的特定联合惩罚，从而得到清晰的投影与近端算子公式。

Result: 数值实验表明，该解耦重构比耦合基线更鲁棒：可用的正则化强度范围更宽、灾难性过度剪枝率更低，同时保持可比精度。在受控分类与稀疏恢复研究中验证了这些性质，并在高维 PINN 压力测试和 OPT-1.3B 的前馈层上考察了其适用范围。

Conclusion: 解耦、逐层的结构化稀疏重构在鲁棒性和可用正则范围上优于耦合联合方法，其等价性结论为投影/近端算法提供了理论基础，该方法在大规模语言模型前馈层和高维 PINN 场景下展现出应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose a decoupled, layerwise method for structurally sparsifying the fully connected layers of pretrained neural networks. Rather than penalizing all layers jointly, our approach extracts shallow two-layer subnetworks, normalizes the inner weights, and applies a structured group penalty to the outer weight matrix of each block, processing layers sequentially to prune neurons and reduce the width of each layer. We prove that the constrained decoupled objective is equivalent at optimality to a specific joint penalty on the inner and outer weights, for any positively homogeneous activation, and thus admits a clean projected and proximal formulation. Our central finding is that this decoupled reformulation is more robust than coupled methods. In numerical experiments it provides a wider usable range of the regularization strength and a lower rate of catastrophic over-pruning than the tested joint baseline while maintaining comparable accuracy. We establish these properties in controlled classification and sparse-recovery studies, and examine their scope in a high-dimensional PINN stress test and in the feed-forward layers of OPT-1.3B.

</details>


### [167] [Stiefel-AdamW: Geometry-Aware AdamW for Linear Factorization Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21039)
*Emanuele Zangrando,Marco Sutti,Francesco Tudisco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Stiefel-AdamW，一种针对线性因式分解块（如 LoRA、低秩压缩层、注意力 QK 乘积）的近即插即用优化器，通过将其中一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形上消解因式分解非唯一性带来的训练不稳定问题，在几乎不增加成本的情况下取得优于 AdamW 的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习普遍存在线性因式分解块 W=BA，其因式分解具有非唯一性，会破坏训练稳定性并限制可用学习率；但现有做法仍用忽略底层几何结构的标准欧氏方法（如 AdamW）优化，未利用流形结构消除这一病态。

Method: 作者提出 Stiefel-AdamW：将一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形上、另一个保持欧氏，从而把完整的 GL(R^r) 规范对称性松弛为紧致的正交对称性，杜绝因子爆炸；同时保留 AdamW 的坐标级对角预处理。矩估计在环境欧氏空间中进行，几何仅通过切空间投影与流形回缩(retraction)引入，实现开销极小。

Result: 所提优化器继承了黎曼方法的稳定性优势与标准收敛保证。在 GPT2、ViT、Mistral 7B 的 LoRA 式微调以及 GPT2 在 OpenWebText 上的全量预训练中，Stiefel-AdamW 均比强基线（如 AdamW）取得一致提升，且成本基本无额外增加。

Conclusion: Stiefel-AdamW 是一种针对线性因式分解块的高效优化器，通过流形约束消除因式分解非唯一性，在保持 AdamW 实用强度的同时显著提升稳定性与性能，可作为 AdamW 的近即插即用替代方案。

Abstract: A pervasive structural pattern in modern deep learning is the linear factorization block: a submodule of the form $W = BA$ in which two parameter matrices are multiplied directly, with no intervening nonlinearity. Such blocks appear in LoRA adapters, low-rank compressed layers, query-key products of self-attention, and share a common pathology: the factorization is non-unique, which can destabilize training and limit usable learning rates. Despite this, factorization blocks are typically optimized with standard Euclidean methods that ignore the underlying geometry. We introduce Stiefel-AdamW, a near drop-in replacement for AdamW for use wherever such blocks appear. By constraining one factor on the Stiefel manifold while leaving the other Euclidean, Stiefel-AdamW relaxes the full $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{R}^r)$ gauge symmetry to a compact orthogonal symmetry, ruling out factor blow-up while retaining the coordinate-wise diagonal preconditioning that gives AdamW its practical strength. Moment estimation is performed in the ambient Euclidean space, with geometry entering only through a tangent-space projection and a manifold retraction. The implementation overhead over AdamW is minimal, and we show that the resulting optimizer inherits both the stability benefits of Riemannian methods and standard convergence guarantees. We validate Stiefel-AdamW on LoRA-style fine-tuning of GPT2, ViT, and Mistral 7B and on full pretraining of GPT2 on OpenWebText, showing consistent improvements over strong baselines at essentially no additional cost over AdamW.

</details>


### [168] [A Lightweight Plug-in Gate for Transformer-Based Time-Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21044)
*Hongkai Zhuang,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究预编码器协变量准入接口，在Transformer预测器编码器前加入轻量级表示级门控（sigmoid评分）和一个使用量正则化变体，在零额外调参协议下作为TimeXer、iTransformer、PatchTST的即插即用模块评估，实验显示门控与基线竞争力相当，使用量惩罚可降低平均准入分数同时保持预测误差接近无惩罚设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量丰富的时间序列预测需要决定外部变量如何进入目标预测路径；现有基于Transformer的预测器通常直接构建协变量表示并传入编码器，缺乏显式的准入（admission）阶段。本文旨在研究编码器处理前对协变量表示进行即时调控的输入端接口。

Method: 实现一个预编码器表示级门控接口，对表示单元赋予sigmoid评分以调控准入；并研究一种使用量正则化变体，惩罚平均准入分数。该接口作为TimeXer、iTransformer、PatchTST的即插即用模块，在零额外调参协议下评估，各门控模型直接继承对应基线配置。

Result: 在ETTm1、ETTm2、Traffic、Energy、ILI数据集上，门控与对应基线具有竞争力；使用量惩罚降低平均准入分数，同时保持预测误差接近无惩罚的TimeXer设定。实验包括配对预测对比、门控位置消融、初始化消融、受控协变量准入分析，以及基于VIF的排列特征重要性（PFI）诊断案例研究。

Conclusion: 预编码器表示级门控为协变量准入提供了一个轻量、可插拔且无需额外调参的输入端接口，在与基线竞争力相当的前提下，使用量正则化能有效降低平均准入分数，为协变量丰富的Transformer预测器提供了一种调控输入表示的实用手段。

Abstract: Covariate-rich time-series forecasting requires deciding how external variables enter the target forecasting path. Existing Transformer-based forecasters usually build a covariate representation and pass it to the encoder without an explicit admission stage. This paper studies pre-encoder covariate admission as an input-side interface that regulates that representation immediately before encoder processing. We implement the interface with a lightweight representation-level pre-encoder gate that assigns sigmoid scores to representation units, and we also study a usage-regularized variant that penalizes average admission. The interface is evaluated as a plug-in module for TimeXer, Inverted Transformer (iTransformer), and Patch Time Series Transformer (PatchTST) under a zero-extra-tuning protocol, where each gated model inherits the corresponding baseline configuration. Experiments on the Electricity Transformer Temperature minute-level (ETTm1 and ETTm2) datasets, Traffic, Energy, and influenza-like illness (ILI) include paired forecasting comparisons, gate-placement ablation, initialization ablation, controlled covariate-admission analysis, and a variance inflation factor (VIF)-informed permutation feature importance (PFI) diagnostic case study. In the tested settings, the gate is competitive with the corresponding baselines, and the usage penalty reduces average admission scores while keeping forecasting errors close to the unpenalized TimeXer setting.

</details>


### [169] [Multi-Domain Clustering via Measure Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21664)
*Rafael Pereira Eufrazio,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Charles Casimiro Cavalcante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于测度量化的多域聚类通用框架，通过最小化各域概率测度与原型测度之间的概率度量（如Sinkhorn散度、MMD）学习共享原型集，并采用小批量优化实现可扩展的拟合与分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类通常依赖K-means等基于质心的方法，但缺乏面向多域数据的通用框架；同时，大规模多域数据在内存和计算上存在可扩展性挑战，需要一种能跨域共享表征、兼具协作性与高效性的聚类方法。

Method: 将多域聚类重新表述为测度量化问题：给定多域样本，通过最小化每个域的概率测度与原型测度之间的概率度量（Sinkhorn散度或最大均值差异MMD）来学习共享的聚类原型集。点分配到簇采用两种策略——最近质心分配，或基于最优传输的协作分配（耦合域内所有样本）。采用小批量优化策略来降低内存与计算开销，使拟合与分配均可扩展。

Result: 在涵盖图像、音频和传感器数据的5个多域基准上，基于Sinkhorn的方法一致优于经典聚类与多域聚类基线；当扩展到数十万样本时，该优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 所提出的测度量化框架为多域聚类提供了通用且可扩展的解决方案，Sinkhorn散度方法在多个基准和规模上均表现优越，验证了概率度量与最优传输策略在多域聚类中的有效性。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental task in data analysis, typically addressed through centroid-based methods such as K-means. In this work, we present a general framework for multi-domain clustering via measure quantization: given samples from multiple domains, we learn a shared set of cluster prototypes by minimizing a probability metric, such as the Sinkhorn divergence or the Maximum Mean Discrepancy, between each domain's probability measure and the measure of prototypes. Data points are then assigned to clusters either via nearest centroid, or via optimal transport, a collaborative strategy that couples all samples within a domain. A mini-batch optimization strategy makes both fitting and assignment scalable, reducing memory and computational cost while preserving clustering performance. Experimental results on 5 multi-domain benchmarks spanning image, audio and sensor data show that our Sinkhorn-based method consistently outperforms classical and multi-domain clustering baselines, and that this advantage persists when scaling to hundreds of thousands of samples.

</details>


### [170] [FedeRage: Provably Convergent Agnostic Federated Learning under General Client Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21057)
*Herlock Rahimi,Dionysis Kalogerias*

Main category: cs.LG
