Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that, while retaining only about 52--65\% of the original teacher--student discrepancy as the optimization signal, Cal-OPD consistently outperforms standard OPD and its variants across model scales.

</details>


### [271] [One Prompt Does Not Fit All: Self-Meta-Evolve for Personalized Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21626)
*Hongliang Li,Lu Wang,Yong Xu,Hanyang Chen,Zhitao Hou,Xiaoting Qin,Song Ge,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Self-Meta-Evolve 分层框架，面向企业信息抽取场景，为每位用户维护专属提示词，并通过"内层编辑提示词+外层进化元提示词"的双循环机制，结合交互反馈持续优化，显著超越现有提示词优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在企业信息抽取中需针对不同用户对同一文档做不同重组，而现有提示词优化方法依赖单一提示词与全局目标，与企业真实的用户异质性错配，因此需要面向用户个体的提示词适配与反馈优化。

Method: 提出 Self-Meta-Evolve 分层框架，为每个用户维护专属结构化提示词，采用双循环过程：内循环基于人格条件化反馈编辑结构化提示词，外循环通过蒸馏成功的编辑模式来进化元提示词本身；并构建基于 O*NET 职业分类的 292 名模拟企业用户的基准及可复现的人格生成管线。

Result: 在该基准上 Self-Meta-Evolve 成功率达 74.58%，比最强提示词优化基线高出 13.56 个绝对百分点，仅两轮迭代即可达到 52.54%；对二十名真实专业人士的双盲人工研究显示，自适应提示词在 71% 的两两比较中胜过静态基线。

Conclusion: Self-Meta-Evolve 通过分层双循环自我进化机制有效解决企业 IE 中的用户异构性问题，在自动评测与人工评测上均显著优于现有方法，实现了可扩展、个性化的提示词持续适配。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for enterprise information extraction (IE), where the same document must be reorganized differently for each user. Existing prompt optimization methods, however, rely on a single prompt optimized against a global objective, which is misaligned with the inherent user heterogeneity of real workplaces. We formulate enterprise IE as per-user prompt adaptation under interaction feedback and propose Self-Meta-Evolve, a hierarchical framework that maintains a dedicated prompt for each user and continuously refines it through a dual-loop process: an inner loop that edits structured prompts based on persona-conditioned feedback, and an outer loop that evolves the meta-prompt itself by distilling successful editing patterns. To enable scalable training and evaluation, we release a persona-driven IE benchmark of 292 simulated enterprise users, paired with a reproducible persona-generation pipeline grounded in O*NET occupational taxonomies. On this benchmark, Self-Meta-Evolve achieves a 74.58% success rate, outperforming the strongest prompt-optimization baseline by 13.56 absolute points, and reaches 52.54\% within only two iterations. A double-blind human study with twenty real professionals further confirms that prompts adapted by our framework win against static baselines in 71% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [272] [Accelerating Dense LLMs via L0-regularized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21672)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Jiudong Yang,Zhaowen Tao,Meng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 L0-MoE，一种利用 L0 正则化的轻量级混合专家（MoE）方法，可在几乎不损失性能的情况下将稠密大语言模型的推理加速最高 2.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然性能强大，但推理速度慢、成本高；现有加速方法往往导致明显的性能下降，而传统 MoE 模型又需要大量计算资源。因此需要一种既能显著加速、又能保持性能且资源开销低的方案。

Method: 提出 L0-MoE 轻量级 MoE 方法，采用 L0 正则化技术对稠密 LLM 进行加速；同时引入"簇混淆矩阵"（cluster confusion matrix）用于领域感知的数据集筛选，并采用动态批处理（dynamic batching）提升训练效率。

Result: 实验表明，L0-MoE 相较稠密模型最高实现 2.5 倍加速，同时保持具有竞争力的性能，并优于现有的 LLM 加速基线方法。

Conclusion: L0-MoE 在几乎不损失性能的前提下实现了对稠密大语言模型的有效加速，为 LLM 推理加速提供了一种轻量且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance but suffer from slow and costly inference. Existing acceleration methods often lead to noticeable performance degradation, while Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models require extensive computational resources. In this paper, we propose L0-MoE, a lightweight MoE approach using L0-regularization to accelerate dense LLMs nearly without performance loss. Our method introduces a cluster confusion matrix for domain-aware dataset curation and applies dynamic batching for efficient training. Experiments show that L0-MoE achieves up to 2.5x speedup over dense models while maintaining competitive performance, outperforming existing LLM acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [273] [GUARD: Natural Forgetting in Large Reasoning Models via Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21677)
*Zeyu Yan,Guanghao Zhou,Minghui Qiu,Ming Gao,Cen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GUARD，一种面向大型推理模型的机器遗忘方法，通过让模型学习“自然遗忘轨迹”（不泄露的思维链 + 稳定的拒绝式回答）来替代原有泄露内容，并配套提出 NFRS 指标评估遗忘后的推理输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LRM 遗忘目标往往只抑制目标内容或重定向内部表征，却没有规定遗忘后的输出轨迹应如何继续，容易导致幻觉式替代、边界畸形或重复输出；且泄露信息可能在中间思维链中提前浮现，使遗忘更困难。

Method: 提出 Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation (GUARD)：将模型生成的不安全泄露转换为“安全退出轨迹”，通过引导标记对齐冻结的 LRM，再把引导后的行为蒸馏进模型参数；并引入 Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score (NFRS) 从结构稳定性、流畅性和无依据替代等维度评估遗忘后输出质量。

Result: 在 R-TOFU 和基于 STAR-1 的有害意图设定上的实验表明，GUARD 在两种广泛采用的蒸馏型 LRM 上显著降低不安全和隐私泄露，同时保持推理效用。

Conclusion: LRM 遗忘应学习连贯的“自然遗忘轨迹”而非简单抑制内容；GUARD 和 NFRS 为提升遗忘质量与评估替代输出提供了有效方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have made machine unlearning more challenging, as protected facts or unsafe rationales may surface in intermediate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before the final answer is produced. Existing unlearning objectives typically suppress the target content or redirect internal representations, but they never specify how the post-forgetting trajectory should continue, which can lead to hallucinated substitutes, malformed boundaries, or repetitive outputs. We argue that LRM unlearning should instead learn a natural forgetting trajectory: a coherent non-disclosing CoT followed by a stable refusal-style answer that replace the original disclosure. To this end, we propose Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation (GUARD), which converts model-generated unsafe disclosures into safe-exit trajectories, aligns a frozen LRM via guidance tokens, and distills the guided behavior into model parameters.To address the lack of metrics for replacement quality beyond leakage, we further introduce Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score (NFRS), which captures structural stability, fluency, and unsupported substitutes in forgotten outputs. Extensive experiments on R-TOFU and a STAR-1-derived harmful-intent setting show that GUARD substantially reduces unsafe and privacy disclosures across two widely adopted distilled LRMs while preserving reasoning utility. Codes are available at https://github.com/zeyu-Yan/GUARD

</details>


### [274] [Listen Before You Speak: Response Planning from Listener Facial Reactions for Conversational Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21683)
*Yunji Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReACT-TTS 两阶段框架，利用听者回应前一秒的面部序列来规划下一句语音的情感与韵律，并在 MELD 对话协议、消融实验和人工适宜性评测中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式语音高度依赖对话上下文和听者即时行为，现有语音合成方法往往忽略听者回应前的动态线索，导致生成的回应在情感和韵律上缺乏上下文适宜性。作者旨在探索“回应前听者动态”是否可作为互补信号来规划对话回应。

Method: 提出 ReACT-TTS 两阶段框架：第一阶段在严格的双人 MELD 协议下，用听者回应前一秒的面部序列进行时间条件建模，规划下一句的情感与韵律（预测回应风格）；第二阶段将该预测的回应风格接入 Grad-TTS 主干实现端到端语音合成。通过十次随机种子对比 Temporal 与 Text-only、进行视觉变体的消融（含显式早晚期差异、循环错配等）验证设计。

Result: 在 MELD 协议下，Temporal 条件相比 Text-only 在十次种子中取得更高的平均宏 F1 和 VAD 一致性，而准确率基本不变；消融显示时间建模在视觉变体中表现最佳，显式早晚期差异非必需，正确的听者反应平均优于循环错配。20 名语音研究者的适宜性测评中，76% 判断偏好 Temporal，9% 偏好 Text-only，15% 无偏好。

Conclusion: 结果支持将“回应前听者动态”作为对话回应规划的互补线索，ReACT-TTS 能够利用听者面部时序信息有效规划情感与韵律并实现端到端语音合成。代码已开源（https://github.com/CYJ1/ReACT-TTS_public）。

Abstract: Conversational speech depends on dialogue context and the listener's immediately preceding behavior. We propose ReACT-TTS, a two-stage framework that uses a one-second pre-response listener facial sequence to plan the next utterance's emotion and prosody before speech realization. On a strict dyadic MELD protocol, Temporal conditioning yields higher mean macro-F1 and VAD concordance than Text-only across ten seeds, while accuracy remains essentially unchanged. Ablations show that temporal modeling performs best among the visual variants and that an explicit early-to-late difference is unnecessary; correct listener reactions also outperform cyclic mismatches on average. In a contextual-appropriateness study with 20 speech researchers, 76% of judgments prefer Temporal, 9% Text-only, and 15% report no preference. We further connect the predicted response style to a Grad-TTS backbone for end-to-end speech realization. Overall, the results support pre-response listener dynamics as complementary cues for conversational response planning. The source code is available at https://github.com/CYJ1/ReACT-TTS_public.

</details>


### [275] [World Modeling in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21748)
*Pierre Beckmann,Matthieu Queloz,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文表明，行为失败不应被简单解释为模型缺乏世界模型。通过对在曼哈顿随机游走路径上训练的 TaxiGPT 进行机制分析，作者证明该模型实际学到了忠实的内部地图（表示路口与街道、追踪位置、使用"目标罗盘"导航），其行为失败源于叠加路口特征间的干扰，可通过"可供性打包"缓解，并提出机制性指标用于比较模型并追踪世界建模能力随训练阶段的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常因模型的行为失败而否认其具备世界模型，这种"以行为成败论有无"的推断可能具有误导性。作者旨在扭转这一范式，主张应从机制层面研究模型是否以及如何表征环境并利用表征指导行为，而非仅凭行为输出判断是否拥有世界模型。

Method: 以在曼哈顿随机游走数据上训练的 TaxiGPT 为对象，开展机制分析与因果干预：识别模型对路口、街道的表示，追踪位置表征，定位"目标罗盘"导航机制；通过分析叠加（superposition）路口特征之间的干扰来解释行为失败；引入"可供性打包"（affordance packing，即将具有相同合法移动的路口表征分组）以限制错误后果；并提出可比较模型的机制性指标。

Result: 模型确实表征了路口与街道、追踪自身位置，并利用目标罗盘导航；其行为失败可追溯至叠加路口特征间的干扰，这种干扰破坏了内部地图中的定位；可供性打包有助于限制这些错误的后果；世界建模能力在不同训练阶段涌现，机制性指标能够用于跨模型比较。

Conclusion: 行为失败可能掩盖真实的世界建模能力，因此研究焦点应从"模型是否有世界模型"转向对世界建模的机制性研究，即考察模型表征环境并利用这些表征引导行为的相互作用的能力。

Abstract: Behavioral failures can make a transformer appear to lack a world model even when it has learned faithful representations of its environment. We demonstrate this in TaxiGPT, a transformer trained on random walks through Manhattan whose failures have been interpreted as evidence of an incoherent internal map. Through mechanistic analysis and causal interventions, we show that the model represents intersections and streets, tracks its position, and uses a goal compass to navigate. We trace its failures to interference between superposed intersection features, which disrupts localization within the internal map. Affordance packing, which groups representations of intersections with the same legal moves, helps limit the consequences of these errors. Finally, we propose mechanistic indicators that we use to compare models and show that world-modeling capacities emerge at different stages of training. Our findings motivate a shift from asking whether a model has a world model to mechanistically studying its world modeling: the interacting capacities through which it represents its environment and uses those representations to guide behavior.

</details>


### [276] [ECG Mirage: Revealing and Mitigating the Underutilisation of ECGs in Vision-Language Models for Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21755)
*Jinning Liang,Mingcheng Zhu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"ECG Mirage"（心电图幻象）这一失败模式，指视觉-语言模型在多模态临床预测中看似有效，实则未真正利用患者特异性心电图信息；并通过视觉提示调优（visual prompt tuning）加以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科决策依赖多模态临床信息，VLM虽能联合处理文本与心电图，但强预测性能并不意味着真正使用了正确患者的心电图。作者旨在揭示并量化这一表面多模态能力背后的隐患。

Method: 定义两类ECG Mirage：ECG忽视（心电图几乎无预测增益）与ECG混淆（匹配心电图优于无图输入，但不优于错配心电图）。在固定临床文本和预测目标的前提下，比较匹配心电图、结局不一致的错配心电图及无图输入的预测差异；在MDS-ED数据上评估四个VLM；随后在冻结VLM主干条件下，用监督学习结合条件式直接偏好优化（conditional DPO）训练四个受限视觉提示。

Result: 在MDS-ED上，匹配心电图对ICU入院和临床恶化预测均未带来一致优势（存在ECG Mirage）。经视觉提示调优后，模型平衡准确率达ICU入院70.6%、恶化67.5%，并将匹配与错配心电图间的性能差距分别扩大至约16.5和5.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究确认了多模态临床预测中的ECG Mirage现象，并证明视觉提示调优是一种高效的缓解策略，可增强模型对患者特异性心电图的真实依赖。

Abstract: Emergency department (ED) decision-making relies on heterogeneous clinical information, including patient history, vital signs, laboratory results, and electrocardiograms (ECGs). Vision--language models (VLMs) can jointly process these modalities, but strong predictive performance does not necessarily imply meaningful use of the correct patient's ECG. We term this failure mode ECG Mirage: apparent multimodal capability without useful dependence on patient-specific ECG information. We distinguish two forms: ECG neglect, where ECGs provide little predictive benefit, and ECG confusion, where matched ECGs outperform no-image inputs but not mismatched ECGs. To evaluate these behaviours, we compare predictions obtained with matched ECGs, outcome-discordant mismatched ECGs, and no-image inputs while holding the clinical text and prediction targets fixed. Across four VLMs on MDS-ED, matched ECGs provide no consistent advantage for either ICU admission or clinical deterioration prediction. We then train four restricted visual prompts using supervised learning followed by conditional direct preference optimisation, while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. The resulting models achieve balanced accuracies of 70.6% for ICU admission and 67.5% for deterioration and increase the matched-versus-mismatched performance gap to approximately 16.5 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively. Overall, our study identifies ECG Mirage in multimodal clinical prediction and introduces visual prompt tuning as an efficient mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [277] [LLM-Generated Feature Pools for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21801)
*Youssef Attia El Hili,Malik Tiomoko,Corinne Ancourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在严格选择协议下，一个简单的统计流水线在单变量时间序列异常检测上能达到多好的效果，并通过消融实验与LLM生成特征池将其提升至匹配排行榜最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一条简单统计流水线（滑动窗口提取统计量+MAD转导评分+每域特征选择）在严格协议下的上限，并定位性能的关键决定因素（候选特征池 vs 搜索/聚合方式）。

Method: 方法包括：滑动窗口提取一小组统计特征；用转导鲁棒（MAD）模型对每个窗口打分；在留出的调优切分上为每个域选择特征子集；消融实验分别改变选择策略、聚合网格和候选池；并用多模态LLM针对每个域以该域的上下文示例窗口为提示生成候选池，再对手工池与生成池的并集进行选择。

Result: 在TSB-AD-U上取得0.529的每序列VUS-PR，超过公开排行榜最佳神经网络（0.45）和统计（0.44）条目，并与最强预训练基础模型相差0.06以内。消融显示：改变选择策略与事后神谕仅使分数变化0.031，改变聚合网格变化0.096，而改变候选池变化0.226。LLM生成的池在匹配选择下与手工池持平，二者覆盖不同域；在其并集上选择在全部12个生成器-种子组合上均优于生成池，将流水线提升至0.588，与排行榜最佳条目持平。

Conclusion: 候选特征池设定性能天花板，对它的搜索只是二阶因素；通过LLM生成与手工池互补的候选特征并取并集，简单统计流水线即可匹配最强排行榜成绩。

Abstract: We study how far a simple statistical pipeline can go on univariate time series anomaly detection under a strict selection protocol. The method extracts a small pool of statistics over sliding windows, scores each window with a transductive robust (MAD) model, and selects a feature subset per domain on a held-out tuning split. On TSB-AD-U it reaches $0.529$ per-series VUS-PR, above the best neural ($0.45$) and statistical ($0.44$) entries on the public leaderboard and within $0.06$ of the strongest pretrained foundation model, several of which use more supervision than ours. Ablations locate the cause: across three selection strategies and a hindsight oracle the score moves by $0.031$, and across the aggregation grid by $0.096$, while changing the candidate pool moves it by $0.226$. The candidate pool sets the ceiling; the search over it is second-order. We therefore generate a pool per domain by prompting a multimodal LLM with in-context example windows from that domain. The generated pools match the hand-crafted one under matched selection, and the two cover different domains: selecting over their union improves on the generated pool in all twelve generator-seed pairs and lifts the pipeline to $0.588$, matching the performance of the best entry on the leaderboard.

</details>


### [278] [MIST: Multimodal Survival Prediction with Genomic-Guided Histology Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21811)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Richard R. Chen,Jana Lipkova,Benedikt Wiestler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIST 提出一种以基因组特征为查询、引导组织学上下文注意力的多模态生存预测方法，通过基因组引导的组织学注意力将分子信息与病理信息融合，在四个外部癌症队列上提升了 C-index。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生存模型虽能结合全切片图像（WSI）与基因组谱的互补预后信息，但面临外部队列偏移和计算复杂度的挑战，现有方法仅在最终阶段合并分别编码的模态，缺乏有效的深度融合。

Method: 提出 MIST 模型：将基因组特征表示为 token，并以其为查询对紧凑的基础模型衍生的组织学上下文 token 进行注意力融合，再进行生存预测。训练结合离散时间生存预测、基因组特征掩码、WSI 丢弃以及配对的 WSI-基因组对比对齐。

Result: 在结肠癌、肾癌、肺癌和胶质母细胞瘤四个外部评估队列中，MIST 在主要对比中相较标准融合基线提升了外部 C-index。

Conclusion: 基因组引导的组织学注意力是一种紧凑且有效的多模态肿瘤学结果预测策略，优于仅末级融合的标准方法。

Abstract: Multimodal survival models can combine complementary prognostic information from whole-slide images and genomic profiles, but effective fusion remains challenging amid external cohort shift and computational complexity. To address these challenges, we propose MIST, multimodal survival prediction with genomic-guided histology attention. MIST represents genomic features as tokens and allows them to query compact foundation-model-derived histology context tokens before survival prediction. This design enriches molecular information with histology context rather than merging separately encoded modalities only at the final stage. Training combines discrete-time survival prediction with genomic feature masking, WSI dropout, and paired WSI-genomics contrastive alignment. Across four external evaluations in colon, renal, lung, and glioblastoma cohorts, MIST improves external C-index over standard fusion baselines in the primary comparisons. These results support genomic-guided histology attention as a compact and effective strategy for multimodal oncology outcome prediction. Our code is available at https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST .

</details>


### [279] [AutoRecLab: Describe the Experiment, Get the Code!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21863)
*Moritz Baumgart,Philipp Meister,Justus Krell,Michael Schmidt,Bela Gipp,Joeran Beel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AutoRecLab，一个基于 Python 的自主推荐系统实验室，能够根据自然语言提示自动完成推荐系统实验，并通过 RAG、静态类型验证和执行引导的树搜索实现从想法到可运行实验的自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统研究的核心环节是实证评估，但将实验设计转化为可执行代码目前仍依赖人工操作，过程繁琐且易出错，亟需自动化手段降低门槛与错误率。

Method: AutoRecLab 接收自然语言研究想法后，自动提取显式实验需求，构建并验证原型，再迭代扩展为完整实验。其工作流结合了检索增强生成（RAG）用于文档查询、静态类型验证，以及执行引导的树搜索（execution-steered tree search）。

Result: 在演示中，AutoRecLab 自主实现了一个显式反馈到隐式反馈转换的研究。在覆盖六种算法和三个数据集的基线对比中，9 次运行有 8 次成功，使用 GPT-5.4-mini 时每次运行平均成本约 1 美元。

Conclusion: AutoRecLab 展示了用大语言模型自主完成端到端推荐系统实验的可行性，在保证较高成功率（8/9）的同时控制了经济成本，为自动化实验平台提供了新范式。

Abstract: Empirical evaluation is central to recommender-systems (RecSys) research, but turning experimental designs into executable code remains a manual and error-prone task. We present AutoRecLab, a Python-based autonomous RecSys lab that automates RecSys experiments from natural-language prompts. Given a research idea, AutoRecLab derives explicit experiment requirements, builds and validates a prototype, and iteratively expands it into the requested full experiment. The workflow combines retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for documentation lookup, static type verification, and execution-steered tree search. In our demonstration, AutoRecLab autonomously implements an explicit-to-implicit feedback conversion study. In a baseline comparison across six algorithms and three datasets, 8 of 9 runs succeed at an average cost of approx- imately $1 per run with GPT-5.4-mini.

</details>


### [280] [What Should We Ask Next? Retrieval-Aware Question Learning under Partial Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21924)
*Lyucheng Qian,John Yuehan Zhang,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAVEL，一个面向交互式行人重识别的检索感知在线强化学习框架，通过完整的问题-回答-检索闭环的排序反馈优化提问策略，在 Interactive-PEDES 上实现五轮交互中逐步提升的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式检索系统通过模仿离线排序的候选 QA 对来训练提问决策，但问题的价值取决于其引发的回答及其对后续检索的影响，候选区分度和感知有用性只能提供弱监督，难以真正优化提问策略。

Method: RAVEL 从有监督的问题生成初始化，直接观察当前 Top-4 候选，并利用完整的问题-回答-检索闭环产生的排序反馈，通过在线强化学习优化问题策略。

Result: 在 Interactive-PEDES 上的实验表明，RAVEL 在五轮交互中持续提升检索性能；进一步分析显示，RAVEL 将提问预算重新分配到局部化的开放属性上，这些属性提供更有用的检索证据，并在初始困难查询上取得最大收益。

Conclusion: 以检索感知的在线强化学习取代模仿学习，能让交互式检索系统学会提出真正有利于检索更新、尤其是针对困难查询的高价值问题，从而获得更强的检索性能。

Abstract: Interactive retrieval under partial evidence is a sequential information-acquisition problem: an agent must decide which question will create the most useful evidence for the next retrieval update. Existing systems train this decision by imitating an offline ordering of candidate QA pairs, although question value is determined by the response it elicits and its downstream effect on retrieval. We establish that candidate discriminativeness and perceived usefulness provide weak supervision for this objective, then introduce RAVEL, a retrieval-aware online reinforcement learning framework for interactive person re-identification. RAVEL initializes from supervised question generation, observes the current Top-4 candidates directly, and optimizes the question policy with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop. Experiments on Interactive-PEDES show that RAVEL delivers progressively stronger retrieval performance across five interaction rounds. Further analysis shows that RAVEL reallocates the questioning budget toward localized open-ended attributes, which provide more useful retrieval evidence and yield the largest gains on initially difficult queries.

</details>


### [281] [AutoViewMem: Self-Configuring Orthogonal Views for Conversational Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21940)
*Zijie Cao,Xijun Qu,Zhicheng Gu,Xiaoshu Chen,Duanyang Yuan,Yanning Hou,Sihang Zhou,Jianxing Gong,Jian Huang,Yang Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoViewMem 是一种面向大语言模型智能体的长期记忆框架，在写入阶段将混合信息主动解耦为低重叠的语义视图，使标准 top-K 检索即可获取聚焦证据，并在 LoCoMo 和 PersonaMem 基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统依赖固定粒度或静态模式，当偏好、事件、约束、时序更新等异构信息混在单一表示中时会产生语义干扰，导致 top-K 检索对噪声敏感、相关证据排名不佳。作者认为应在写入时而非检索时完成语义解耦。

Method: 提出 AutoViewMem：从交互轨迹中发现候选语义视图，选出一组紧凑互补的视图，再用这些视图引导写入时的结构化抽取，生成带来源（provenance）依据的记忆；额外采用离线整合（offline consolidation）提升记忆紧凑性与一致性，保持无显式路由、无迭代检索的简单推理流程。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 PersonaMem 基准上，分别基于 Qwen3-8B 与 Qwen3-14B 骨干模型，AutoViewMem 在长时程问答和个性化任务上均优于强记忆基线，同时保持了简单的推理管线。

Conclusion: 将语义解耦从检索阶段前移到写入阶段的表示优先设计，配合离线整合，能有效缓解语义干扰、提升检索聚焦度，从而改善长期记忆智能体的一致性与个性化表现。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for large language model (LLM) agents to maintain consistency and personalization over extended interactions. Existing memory systems typically rely on fixed granularities or static schemas, but these designs struggle when heterogeneous information, such as preferences, events, constraints, and temporal updates, is embedded in a single mixed representation. The resulting semantic interference makes top-K retrieval sensitive to noise and often leaves relevant evidence poorly ranked. We present AutoViewMem, a data-driven framework that organizes long-term conversational memory into self-configuring, low-overlap semantic views before indexing. AutoViewMem discovers candidate views from interaction traces, selects a compact complementary view set, and uses these views to guide write-time structured extraction of provenance-grounded memories. This representation-first design moves semantic disentanglement from retrieval time to write time, allowing standard top-K similarity search to retrieve focused evidence without explicit routing or iterative retrieval. We further apply offline consolidation to improve memory compactness and consistency. Experiments on the LoCoMo and PersonaMem benchmarks, under both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B backbones, show that AutoViewMem improves long-horizon question answering and personalization over strong memory baselines while preserving a simple inference pipeline.

</details>


### [282] [Learning Cardiac Features: ECG Biometrics Across Time and~Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21962)
*Luca Thiebaud,Paul Chauchat,Mustapha Ouladsine,Stéphane Delliaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次在运动应激与跨会话变异等真实条件下系统评估ECG生物特征识别，提出的Siamese ResNet结合晚期多导联融合，在运动与时间感知协议及公开基准上取得了先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ECG生物特征研究大多局限于单会话、静息态数据，对时间与生理变化的鲁棒性缺乏验证。作者旨在填补这一空白，在包含运动诱发应激和跨会话变异的真实条件下评估ECG生物特征识别的稳健性。

Method: 采用带晚期多导联融合策略的Siamese ResNet，在从心肺运动测试提取的大规模ECG数据集上训练，并通过运动与时间感知的评估协议及公开基准（如CYBHi）进行验证。

Result: 会话内静息至峰值（rest-to-peak）的等错误率（EER）为1.7%，在CYBHi数据集上取得当前最优的3.9% EER。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持存在一种对生理与时间漂移具有鲁棒性的内在心电"签名"，表明ECG生物特征识别在现实条件下具有可行性。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) carry subject-specific patterns enabling reliable individual discrimination, forming the basis of ECG biometrics. Beyond authentication, this paradigm holds significant potential to secure sensitive cardiac data and to serve as a pretext task in self-supervised learning. Yet, most studies remain confined to singlesession, resting data, leaving robustness to temporal and physiological variations largely untested. We address this gap by evaluating ECG biometrics under realistic conditions involving exercise-induced stress and cross-session variability. A Siamese ResNet with late multi-lead fusion strategy is trained on a large ECG dataset extracted from cardiopulmonary exercise tests and evaluated with a exercise-and time-aware protocol, as well as on public benchmarks. This first extensive assessment of ECG biometrics under combined physiological and temporal variability achieves an intra-session rest-to-peak EER of 1.7% and stateof-the-art 3.9% on the CYBHi dataset. Findings support the presence of an intrinsic cardiac signature resilient to physiological and temporal drift.

</details>


### [283] [A Lie Detector Test for Language Models: Reading Knowledge a Model Won't Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21996)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PIR（Probe of Internal Recognition，内部识别探测）方法，借鉴法医学中的隐匿信息测试（CIT），通过读取模型内部状态来判断模型"认得"哪个候选答案，从而区分模型"不愿回答"与"无法回答"，可用于沙袋（sandbagging）审计与遗忘（unlearning）验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能隐藏其内部拥有的知识——例如在能力评估中故意"沙袋"、或回答与内部所知相悖。仅凭模型输出无法判断它是故意隐瞒还是真的不知道。因此需要一种不依赖外部参考、能从模型内部识别"已认知答案"的方法，以支持安全审计与遗忘验证。

Method: 受法医学隐匿信息测试（CIT）启发，PIR 将问题与候选答案一并呈现给模型，通过读取模型内部状态判断模型"识别"出哪个候选为正确答案。PIR 是"无参考"的：不需要诚实的参考模型，也不需要标注真实答案的语料库。该信号具有因果性，并在黑盒行为线索之上提供了额外信息，且从选择题扩展到自由生成。

Result: 在来自五个家族（Gemma、Qwen、Llama、Mistral、Phi）的八个模型上，PIR 以 0.70–0.87 的平衡准确率恢复被识别答案，远高于 0.28–0.40 的未知项基线和 0.25 的随机概率。在各类隐瞒形式（提示性欺骗、训练性沙袋、外部密码锁定、电路断裂检查点）下仍可读取，识别率在 0.85–0.93 之间。当模型隐藏已知答案时识别率保持高位；当遗忘（unlearning）真正移除知识时，识别率降至模型从未学过的水平。

Conclusion: PIR 能够区分"不愿回答"的模型与"无法回答"的模型，为沙袋审计和遗忘验证提供了可靠工具。其信号具有因果性且超越黑盒行为线索，并能从多项选择扩展到自由生成场景。

Abstract: Large language models can hold knowledge they do not report. A model may sandbag on a capability evaluation, or answer against what it internally knows, and its outputs alone cannot tell whether it is hiding an answer or simply does not have one. We borrow the Concealed Information Test, a forensic method that identifies guilty knowledge by presenting a suspect with the true detail among plausible decoys and measuring a stronger response to the item they recognize. Our method, Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR), does the same inside a model. It presents a question with its candidate answers and reads, from the model's internal states, which candidate the model recognizes as correct. PIR is reference-free, needing no honest reference model and no labeled truth corpus. Across eight models from five families (Gemma, Qwen, Llama, Mistral, and Phi), PIR recovers the recognized answer at 0.70 to 0.87 balanced accuracy, well above the 0.28 to 0.40 unknown-item baseline and the 0.25 chance rate. It stays readable across every form of concealment we test, from prompted deception and trained sandbagging to external password-locked and circuit-broken checkpoints, with recognition between 0.85 and 0.93. When the model hides a known answer, recognition stays high. When unlearning removes the knowledge, recognition drops to the level of a question the model never knew. PIR therefore separates a model that will not answer from one that cannot, which supports sandbagging audits and unlearning verification. The signal is causal, adds information beyond black-box behavioral cues, and extends from multiple-choice questions to free-form generation.

</details>


### [284] [CodeMidas: Scaling Agentic Coding RL Environments from Code Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22068)
*Bowen Ye,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Zihao Yue,Linghao Zhang,Hanglong Lv,Yuanxin Liu,Wenhan Ma,Hao Tian,Rang Li,Jinhao Dong,Yikai Zhao,Xiangwei Deng,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Qi Liu,Lingpeng Kong,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CodeMidas，一种仅依赖源代码、通过智能体流水线将现有代码库中已实现的功能自动转化为可执行强化学习任务环境的框架，生成 5,545 个跨语言训练任务，使训练出的 MiMo-V2.5 在五个基准上全线提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的编码智能体训练依赖开发工件（如 issue、commit）来提取任务，可用任务范围受限；作者希望挖掘开源代码库中已实现的功能，以源代码作为唯一输入，实现 RL 环境的大规模、可扩展构建。

Method: 提出 CodeMidas 智能体流水线，在环境构建的每个阶段投入智能体计算：智能体探索已实现功能以形成行为规范、基于原代码执行来构建测试、通过执行检查和重复解决方案 rollouts 验证并过滤候选任务。随后用生成的 5,545 个任务（来自 3,185 个开源代码库、23 种编程语言、15 个技术领域）以 GRPO 训练 MiMo-V2.5。

Result: 生成的训练集含 5,545 个任务，覆盖面广。经 CodeMidas 任务训练的 MiMo-V2.5 在五个不同基准上均有提升：issue 修复 DeepSWE +11.7%、整程序构建 ProgramBench +17%、终端任务 Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%。消融实验显示高质量训练任务数量增加能提升性能；轨迹分析表明 RL 训练后的智能体展现出更充分的代码库探索和更多样化的自我验证等更优行为。

Conclusion: 源代码可以作为构建 RL 环境的可扩展基础，CodeMidas 表明从已实现功能生成任务的思路能有效提升编码智能体在多种软件任务上的表现，为大规模 RL 环境构建开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Training capable coding agents via reinforcement learning (RL) requires diverse tasks with reliable verifiers. Open-source codebases offer a rich source of such tasks, while existing methods typically rely on development artifacts such as issues and commits, limiting the range of tasks that can be extracted. To better scale RL environments, we present CodeMidas, an agentic pipeline that turns implemented functionality in existing codebases into executable RL environments using source code as its only task-specific input. CodeMidas allocates agentic compute to every stage of environment construction: agents explore implemented functionality to formulate behavioral specifications, construct tests grounded in execution of the original code, and validate and filter candidate tasks through execution checks and repeated solution rollouts. The resulting dataset has 5,545 training tasks from 3,185 open-source codebases spanning 23 programming languages and 15 technical domains. Training MiMo-V2.5 on these tasks with GRPO improves performance on all five diverse benchmarks, covering issue repair (DeepSWE + 11.7%), whole-program construction (ProgramBench +17%), and terminal work (Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%). Ablations show that increasing the number of high-quality training tasks improves performance. Trajectory analysis shows the RL-trained agent demonstrates better behaviors like increasing codebase exploration and more diverse self-verification. These results establish source code as a scalable foundation for constructing RL environments that improve coding agents across diverse software tasks.

</details>


### [285] [Designer-RSI: Evolving Procedural Memory from User Traffic for Agentic Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22086)
*Hongyang Du,Lan Yan,Christian Flores,Asim Kadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对专业平面设计的持续适应框架：冻结的前沿模型通过230多个工具操作设计软件，并借助外部"程序性记忆"（自然语言技能库）从经验中积累和精炼可复用设计流程。记忆通过"拓宽"（新增流程）和"深化"（依据成败修订现有流程）演化，配合回放门控只接受既修复失败又不回退已有成功的修改。5轮、1406个真实用户需求与1869条自动评分轨迹上，无需权重更新和人工标注，技能库从76增至139，GenEval2执行成功率从72.7%升至99.3%，并在多个基准上显著胜出；拓宽与深化联合使用效果优于单独使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业平面设计是长时程智能体任务，结构化可编辑工件由大量相互依赖的动作产生，但结果缺乏可靠的程序化评判（oracle）——反馈噪声大且不可验证。这使传统基于权重更新或人工标注的持续学习方法难以适用，因此需要一种能在无监督、噪声反馈下持续改进智能体能力的机制。

Method: 提出持续适应框架：冻结前沿模型作为执行器，调用230多个工具操作专业设计软件；外部"程序性记忆"存储自然语言形式的可复用设计技能。记忆演化分两个机制：拓宽（widening）为反复出现的未覆盖子任务获取新流程；深化（deepening）依据流程自身成功与失败的执行记录修订现有流程；同时引入匹配的回放门控（matched replay gate），仅接受能修复失败且不对已观察到的成功产生回退的修改。系统在5轮迭代中处理1406个真实用户需求、1869条自动评分轨迹，全程无权重更新、无人工标签。

Result: 技能库从76条文档派生的技能增长到139条；GenEval2上Claude-Sonnet-4的执行成功率从72.7%提升至99.3%（生成质量+11.99分）；在四个专业设计基准上，Claude-Sonnet-4与Claude-Opus-4.6相对无技能智能体的胜率分别为61.8%和67.6%。在200个留出需求（用户流量基准）上，仅拓宽或仅深化分别取得49.4%和48.6%的胜率，两者结合达58.5%（p=0.025），证明两机制联合有效。

Conclusion: 程序性记忆为智能体在噪声大、反馈不可验证条件下的持续适应提供了一条实用路径，无需权重更新或人工标注即可持续提升专业设计任务的执行能力。

Abstract: Professional graphic design is a long-horizon agentic task in which structured, editable artifacts emerge from many interdependent actions, yet outcomes admit no reliable programmatic oracle. We introduce a continual adaptation framework in which a frozen frontier model operates professional design software through more than 230 tools, while an external procedural memory of natural-language skills accumulates and refines reusable design procedures from experience. The memory widens by acquiring procedures for recurring uncovered subtasks and deepens by revising existing procedures against their own successful and failed executions, while a matched replay gate admits only changes that repair failures without regressing observed successes. Five rounds over 1,406 real user briefs and 1,869 automatically graded trajectories, with no weight updates and no human labels, grow the bank from 76 documentation-derived skills to 139 and raise GenEval2 execution success on Claude-Sonnet-4 from 72.7% to 99.3% (+11.99 points in generation quality), with 61.8% and 67.6% win rates against the no-skill agent across four specialized design benchmarks on Claude-Sonnet-4 and Claude-Opus-4.6. We further show the two mechanisms are effective in combination: on 200 held-out briefs from user-traffic benchmark, widening or deepening alone reaches a 49.4% / 48.6% win rate over the no-skill agent, while their combination reaches 58.5% (p = 0.025). Procedural memory offers a practical route to continual adaptation of agents under noisy, unverifiable feedback.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Identification problem and quasi-maximum likelihood estimation for matrix-variate CP-factor models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20910)
*Hanzi Ye,Chun Yip Yau*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文对矩阵值时间序列的矩阵变量CP因子模型的可识别性问题给出统一处理，刻画参数空间为可识别与不可识别两个子空间的并集，并提出拟极大似然估计(QMLE)以在整个可识别子空间上获得一致估计，且载荷矩阵收敛速率更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵值时间序列在经济学、神经科学、推荐系统等领域日益重要，CP因子模型是捕捉矩阵时间序列低秩结构的重要模型类，但其可识别性问题缺乏统一处理，现有估计方法的适用范围存在局限。

Method: 作者综合运用解析与代数工具，将CP因子模型的参数空间刻画为可识别子空间与不可识别子空间的并集；证明现有估计方法仅适用于可识别子空间的一个真开子集；进而提出拟极大似然估计(QMLE)程序，以实现整个可识别子空间上的一致估计。

Result: 研究表明现有估计方法只适用于可识别子空间的真开子集，而所提出的QMLE可在整个可识别子空间上实现一致估计，且估计的载荷矩阵相比现有方法具有更快的收敛速率。模拟研究与实际应用验证了该方法在有限样本下的表现。

Conclusion: 本文对CP因子模型的可识别性提供了统一的理论刻画，指出并解决了现有方法适用范围不足的问题；提出的QMLE方法在更广的可识别子空间上一致且收敛更快，具有良好的有限样本性能。

Abstract: Matrix-valued time series, arising in diverse fields such as economics, neuroscience, and recommender systems, have become increasingly prominent in modern data analysis. Among various modeling frameworks, the matrix-variate CP-factor model represents an important and widely applicable class for capturing low-rank structures in matrix time series. In this paper, we provide a unified treatment for the identification problem of CP factor models using both analytic and algebraic tools. In particular, we characterize the parameter space as the union of two subspaces, one identifiable and the other non-identifiable. We show that existing estimation methods only apply to an open proper subset of the identifiable subspace. In contrast, we propose a quasi-maximum likelihood estimation (QMLE) procedure for CP factor models, which allows consistent estimation on the whole identifiable subspace. Moreover, we show that the estimated loading matrices by QMLE achieve a faster convergence rate compared to existing approaches. A simulation study and a real application are conducted to demonstrate the finite-sample performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [287] [Aggregated Posterior Predictive Checks for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20999)
*Shweta Dutta,Gemma E. Moran*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 传统潜变量生成模型常因简单先验与聚合后验不匹配而导致生成质量差，本文提出聚合后验预测检查（APPC），从理论和实验上验证了用聚合后验替代先验进行抽样的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜变量生成模型通常采用简单先验（如高斯先验）进行拟合，但简单先验的抽样常与聚合后验（由模型与数据诱导的潜变量分布）不匹配，导致生成数据不真实。现有做法常将其归因于先验设定错误并主张替换先验；而现代生成模型越来越多采用两阶段策略：先拟合模型，再估计聚合后验，并从聚合后验采样生成数据。作者旨在为这类“从聚合后验采样”的程序建立一套检验与理论保障。

Method: 作者提出聚合后验预测检查（APPC）作为检验工具。理论上，给出了 APPC 渐近校准（asymptotically calibrated）的充分条件，并针对概率主成分分析（PPCA）证明：即使潜先验设定错误，只要普遍因子（pervasive factors）允许恢复信号空间，APPC 仍能保持校准。实验上，在变分自编码器（VAE）上比较聚合后验采样与高斯先验采样。

Result: 理论结果：给出了 APPC 渐近校准的充分条件；在 PPCA 中，即使潜先验错误设定，只要普遍因子足以恢复信号空间，APPC 依然校准。实验结果：在重尾数据和聚类数据上，聚合后验采样相较高斯先验采样改善了生成效果，且表现可与采用更灵活潜先验的模型相媲美。

Conclusion: APPC 为“估计并采样聚合后验而非传统先验”的两阶段生成策略提供了有效的检验机制与理论支持；在数据分布与简单先验不匹配（如重尾、聚类）时，聚合后验采样能显著提升生成质量，是一种可行且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Latent variable generative models are commonly fit using simple priors over latent variables, but draws from these priors often fail to produce realistic data. This failure is due to a mismatch between the prior and the aggregated posterior, the distribution of latent variables induced by the fitted model and the data. This mismatch is often viewed as evidence that the prior is misspecified and should be replaced. Alternatively, in modern generative models, a two-stage strategy is increasingly used where first, the model is fit, and second, the aggregated posterior is estimated (van den Oord et al.,2017; Rombach et al., 2022.). Synthetic data are then obtained by sampling from this aggregated posterior instead of the prior. To check such procedures, we introduce the aggregated posterior predictive check (APPC). Theoretically, we establish sufficient conditions under which the APPC is asymptotically calibrated. For probabilistic principal component analysis, we show that the APPC can remain calibrated under a misspecified latent prior when pervasive factors permit recovery of the signal space. Experiments with variational autoencoders show that aggregated posterior sampling improves generation for heavy-tailed and clustered data relative to Gaussian prior sampling while performing comparably to models with more flexible latent priors.

</details>


### [288] [A New Generalized Birnbaum-Saunders Regression Model: Inference, Diagnostics, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21063)
*Matheus B. Milhomem,Terezinha K. A. Ribeiro,Michelli Barros,Eriton B. Santos*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向广义Birnbaum-Saunders（GBS）分布的新回归模型，采用GAMLSS框架将协变量效应引入全部三个参数，其中某一参数可直接解释为响应变量的中位数。模型通过最大似然估计，并基于Wald检验提出假设检验，并在R的gamlss包中实现。模拟研究与两个真实数据应用验证了该模型的良好性能与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Birnbaum-Saunders（BS）分布广泛用于正且不对称的连续数据，已有多种扩展与回归形式，但具有更强物理解释的广义Birnbaum-Saunders（GBS）分布在文献中受到的关注有限。因此，本文希望针对GBS分布建立灵活的回归模型，引入协变量效应，以提升对这类数据的建模能力。

Method: 本文在广义可加模型（GAMLSS）框架下构建GBS回归模型，将协变量效应引入分布的全部三个参数（位置、尺度、形状），其中一个参数直接解释为响应变量的中位数。参数估计采用最大似然方法，假设检验基于Wald检验，并在R的gamlss包中实现，以便使用模型拟合、诊断与评估等工具。通过蒙特卡洛模拟研究考察估计量的有限样本表现及Wald检验的行为，并用两个真实数据应用进行实证验证。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟表明，最大似然估计量具有良好的性质：随样本量增大，偏差减小、效率提高；Wald检验在中到大样本下表现良好。两个真实数据应用显示，所提出的GBS回归模型相较于BS模型是建模正且不对称数据的灵活替代方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的GBS回归模型通过GAMLSS框架在分布全部参数中引入协变量，兼具物理解释（中位数解释）与灵活性，最大似然估计和Wald检验表现良好，并通过模拟与实证证明了其作为BS模型替代方案的实用价值。

Abstract: The Birnbaum-Saunders (BS) distribution has become a widely used model for positive and asymmetric continuous data. Several extensions of the BS distribution have been proposed, including regression formulations that relate its parameters to covariates. In this paper, we introduce a new regression model for the Generalized Birnbaum-Saunders (GBS) distribution, an extension that has received limited attention in the literature despite offering a stronger physical interpretation. The proposed framework follows the structure of Generalized Additive Models for Location, Scale, and Shape (GAMLSS), allowing covariate effects to be incorporated into all three parameters of the distribution, thereby providing greater flexibility for modeling data. In particular, one of the model parameters has a direct interpretation as the median of the response variable, facilitating the interpretation of covariate effects on the conditional median. Parameter estimation is performed via maximum likelihood, and hypothesis tests are proposed based on the Wald test. The proposed regression model is implemented in R through the gamlss package, allowing access to a broad range of tools for model fitting, diagnostics, and assessment. Monte Carlo simulation studies are conducted to investigate the finite-sample performance of the maximum likelihood estimators. The results show that the estimators exhibit desirable properties, with bias decreasing and efficiency increasing as sample size grows. Additionally, the behavior of the Wald test is investigated, showing good performance for moderate to large sample sizes. Finally, two applications to real data illustrate the practical usefulness of the proposed GBS regression model, demonstrating that it is a flexible alternative for modeling positive and asymmetric data compared to the BS model

</details>


### [289] [Bayesian additive regression trees for evaluating treatment benefit predictors using observational data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21097)
*Yuan Xia,Mohsen Sadatsafavi,Jeenat Mehareen,Paul Gustafson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于贝叶斯加性回归树（BART）的方法，利用观察性数据评估预设的治疗获益预测器（TBP）的性能，并通过模拟研究和COPD病例研究验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗获益预测器（TBP）在用于患者治疗决策前，必须在目标人群中接受评估。然而在仅有观察性数据可用时，治疗分配并非随机，评估TBP需要满足因果识别的标准假设。现有方法难以在观察性数据背景下同时灵活建模、校正混杂并刻画不确定性。

Method: 利用贝叶斯加性回归树（BART）对预测性能指标的后验分布进行估计，以评估预设的TBP；采用获益集中度（C_b）指数和中度校准曲线（moderate calibration curve）等度量及图形可视化进行评估。通过二分类和连续结局的模拟研究（包括二分类情境下平衡与不平衡的治疗分配）验证方法有效性，并在病例研究中评估COPD患者全身性抗生素治疗的TBP。

Result: 模拟研究证实了所提方法的有效性。病例研究发现，所构建的COPD抗生素治疗TBP实际未能做出校准的预测：它既对风险给出了过于乐观的预测，又夸大了治疗带来的风险降低幅度。

Conclusion: 灵活的贝叶斯方法具备评估治疗获益预测器的潜力，能够支持灵活模型设定、混杂校正和不确定性刻画。

Abstract: A treatment benefit predictor (TBP) is an algorithm that maps a patient's characteristics to their putative benefit from a given treatment, which can be used to inform treatment decisions. However, a TBP must be evaluated in the target population before being adopted for patient care. When only observational data are available, evaluating TBPs requires standard causal identification assumptions, as treatment assignment in such settings is not random. We obtain the posterior distributions of predictive performance measures to evaluate prespecified TBPs using observational data, by taking advantage of Bayesian additive regression trees (BART). We illustrate the evaluation of TBPs using selected measures and graphical visualizations: the concentration of benefit ($C_b$) index and the moderate calibration curve. Simulation studies of binary and continuous outcomes settings, including balanced and imbalanced treatment allocation in the binary setting, establish the validity of the proposed approach. In a case study, we use this approach to assess a TBP for systemic antibiotic therapy for patients with chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD). We show that the constructed TBP does not in fact make calibrated predictions, because it both makes optimistic predictions of risks and exaggerates the risk reduction due to treatment. We conclude that flexible Bayesian approaches have the potential to assess TBPs, offering opportunities for flexible model specifications, adjustment for confounding, and uncertainty characterization.

</details>


### [290] [Where quantum and quantum-like methods earn their place in behavioural-trial analysis: three simulation pilots across the RCT pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21269)
*Giuseppe Alessandro Veltri*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文对行为科学随机对照试验（RCT）分析流程进行阶段性考察，通过三个受控模拟试点评估量子或类量子计算相对于强经典基线的适用性，得出"条件性胜出、表征性胜出、诚实落败"三种结果，并提出采纳/试点/暂缓的决策规则与五项研究缺口议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算已通过舆论动力学演示和决策理论形式化进入行为科学领域，但RCT分析流程本身尚未被检验。作者旨在逐阶段回答量子或类量子计算在何处、相对强经典基线下真正有优势。

Method: 在RCT分析流程的三个阶段分别开展受控模拟试点：（1）功效计算试点——以97结局预处理多重宇宙的真实行为RCT为锚点，比较量子振幅估计与经典抽样蒙特卡洛在匹配查询预算下的表现；（2）干扰试点——采用纠缠结构估计器，对四向溢出效应进行表征；（3）适应试点——用类量子启发的情境老虎机与正确设定的经典基线对比。

Result: （1）在高效态制备条件下，两个非退化结局上量子振幅估计恢复枚举的多重宇宙显著性分数，绝对误差比匹配查询预算的经典抽样蒙特卡洛小20-30倍；（2）纠缠结构估计器能恢复成对模型在任何样本量下都无法表征的四向溢出；（3）类量子启发情境老虎机被正确设定的经典基线决定性击败。

Conclusion: 三种结果——一次条件性胜出、一次表征性胜出、一次诚实落败——共同形成一个关于何时采用、试点或暂缓量子方法的决策规则，并配套提出五项缺口研究议程。

Abstract: Behavioural randomized controlled trials (RCTs) are the empirical workhorse of behavioural science, yet quantum computing has reached the field only through opinion-dynamics demonstrations and decision-theory formalism, leaving the RCT analysis pipeline unexamined. I ask, stage by stage, where quantum or quantum-like computation earns its place against a strong classical baseline, and report three controlled simulation pilots across the pipeline. In a power-calculation pilot anchored on a 97-outcome preprocessing multiverse of real behavioural RCTs, quantum amplitude estimation recovers enumerated multiverse-significance fractions with absolute error 20-30 times smaller than classical sampled Monte Carlo at matched query budgets on the two non-degenerate outcomes, conditional on efficient state preparation. In an interference pilot, an entanglement-structured estimator recovers a four-way spillover that a pairwise model cannot represent at any sample size. In an adaptation pilot, a quantum-inspired contextual bandit is decisively beaten by a correctly specified classical baseline. The three results - one conditional win, one representational win, one honest loss - yield a decision rule for when quantum methods are worth adopting, piloting, or deferring, together with a five-gap research agenda.

</details>


### [291] [Asymptotic Anytime-Valid Quantile Inference under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21338)
*Leheng Cai,Qirui Hu,Shuyuan Wu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种本地差分隐私下的在线分位数推断方法，将随机化响应与动态链式并行随机梯度下降结合，构造出具有强高斯逼近性质的 Polyak-Ruppert 估计量，并利用跨链二次统计量一致估计极限方差，从而得到渐近置信序列与时间一致覆盖，并推广至分位数最优臂识别与 A/B 检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地差分隐私下顺序分位数推断困难，原因在于：每条记录在到达分析者之前已被随机化，且极限分位数方差依赖于未知密度。现有方法缺乏无需单独在线密度估计即可实现有效时间一致推断的手段。

Method: 提出在线方法，将随机化响应（randomized response）与动态链式并行随机梯度下降（P-SGD）结合，得到满足强高斯逼近的 Polyak-Ruppert 估计量；设计完全由私有迭代值计算的跨链二次统计量，一致估计极限方差，无需单独的在线密度估计器；并在多项式链增长条件下支持渐近时间一致覆盖，进一步推广到臂式构造以支持分位数最优臂识别、时间一致简单遗憾界及分位数处理效应的序贯 A/B 检验。

Result: 所得估计量具有强高斯逼近性质；跨链统计量可一致估计极限方差，从而获得渐近置信序列；在多项式链增长下实现渐近时间一致覆盖；臂式构造给出本地私有的分位数最优臂识别、时间一致简单遗憾界及序贯 A/B 检验的理论保证。仿真与薪资数据分析验证了方法的有限样本表现与实用性。

Conclusion: 通过随机化响应与动态链式并行随机梯度下降的结合，本文解决了本地差分隐私下顺序分位数推断中未知密度导致的方差估计难题，提供了严格的理论保证与置信序列，并将框架有效扩展到最优臂识别与 A/B 检验等序贯决策场景，具有理论与实用价值。

Abstract: Sequential quantile inference is difficult under local differential privacy because every record is randomized before reaching the analyst and the limiting quantile variance depends on an unknown density. We develop an online procedure that combines randomized response with dynamically chained parallel stochastic gradient descent (P-SGD). The resulting Polyak--Ruppert estimator admits a strong Gaussian approximation. A cross-chain quadratic statistic, computed entirely from private iterates, consistently estimates the limiting variance without a separate online density estimator. These results yield asymptotic confidence sequences and, under polynomial chain growth, asymptotic time-uniform coverage. Arm-wise constructions support locally private quantile best-arm identification, time-uniform simple-regret bounds, and sequential A/B tests of quantile treatment effects. Simulations and salary-data analyses illustrate the finite-sample behavior and practical use of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [292] [Robust Dual-Regularized Variable Selection under Outlier Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21342)
*Abdul-Nasah Soale,Adewale F. Lukman,Essoham Ali*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段稀疏中位数梯度外积（smOPG）方法，用于在存在离群点污染的单指标模型中实现变量选择，兼具对响应离群点和高杠杆点的稳健性，并通过理论、模拟和实际应用加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据中常含有异常观测，其会对变量选择产生不成比例的影响，尤其是在预测变量结构复杂（如单指标模型）时。现有的单指标模型变量选择方法在离群点污染（响应离群点与高杠杆点）下表现不稳定，因此需要一种稳健的变量选择方法。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：第一阶段通过 ℓ1 惩罚的局部中位数回归估计稀疏局部梯度；第二阶段对所得梯度矩阵进行秩一稀疏近似（正则化奇异值分解）以恢复活跃预测变量集合。中位数回归与局部加权相结合，提供对响应离群点和杠杆点的双重稳健性。

Result: 在不同维度和不同污染机制下的大量模拟表明，smOPG 的变量选择性能优于现有方法；在空气污染和基因组数据上的应用展示了其实用价值。

Conclusion: smOPG 方法无需单个局部回归满足选择一致性即可恢复活跃集合，在离群点污染情形下实现了稳健且可靠的变量选择，具有理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Real data often contain unusual observations that can exert disproportionate effects on variable selection, especially in complex predictor settings. We propose a two-stage {\it sparse median outer product of gradients (smOPG)} method for variable selection in single index models with outlier contamination. We first estimate sparse local gradients via \(\ell_1\)-penalized local median regression and then recover the active predictor set from a rank-one sparse approximation of the resulting gradient matrix using regularized singular value decomposition. The combination of median regression and local weighting provides robustness to both response outliers and leverage points. Extensive simulations across varying dimensions and contamination mechanisms demonstrate the favorable variable selection performance of smOPG relative to existing methods. Applications to air pollution and genomic data demonstrate practical utility, while theory establishes active-set recovery without requiring selection consistency of individual local regressions.

</details>


### [293] [Robust tests for log-normal lifetimes under cyclic-stress accelerated tests and interval monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21434)
*María Jaenada,Leandro Pardo,Kiran Prajapat*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对循环应力加速寿命试验（CyALT）中区间监测的失效计数数据，提出基于加权最小密度幂散度（MDPDE）估计量的稳健 Wald 型与 Rao 型假设检验，以应对数据污染对经典极大似然检验的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环应力加速寿命试验下的产品（如电池充放电、热/压循环部件）通常仅在固定时间点检查，得到区间失效计数而非精确寿命。经典检验依赖极大似然估计，对污染高度敏感；由于区间概率跨应力条件联合估计，单个异常失效计数即可扭曲整个参数向量，严重破坏检验的显著性水平和功效，因此需要稳健的检验方法。

Method: 针对区间监测下的对数正态 CyALT 寿命数据，以加权最小密度幂散度估计量为基础，分别推导简单零假设和复合零假设下的 Wald 型与 Rao 型检验统计量，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估其性能，最后用空调蒸发器寿命数据进行实证。

Result: 蒙特卡洛研究表明，在污染条件下经典检验的显著性水平迅速失控，而所提稳健检验的显著性水平保持接近名义水平，且在干净数据下仅损失少量功效。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于加权 MDPDE 的 Wald 型与 Rao 型稳健检验能有效抵御污染，在维持名义显著性水平的同时保有较高功效，适用于循环应力加速寿命试验区间数据。

Abstract: Some products are, by nature, designed to operate under stress conditions that repeatedly cycle between two levels, such as batteries undergoing repeated charge and discharge, or components exposed to recurring thermal or pressure cycles. When such products are tested under accelerated conditions, the applied stress is naturally elevated in the same cyclic manner rather than held constant or increased in a single step. Cyclic-stress accelerated life testing (CyALT) reflects this by alternating the applied stress between specified levels. Units are often inspected only at fixed times, so the data consist of interval failure counts rather than exact lifetimes. Hypothesis testing plays a crucial role in this setting. It can assess whether the applied stress significantly affects lifetime, or validate the parameter values used to design the experiment. Classical tests rely on the maximum likelihood estimator, which is highly sensitive to contamination. Since interval probabilities are estimated jointly across stress conditions, a single anomalous failure count can distort the entire parameter vector and severely affect the test's significance level and power. This paper develops Wald-type and Rao-type test statistics for log-normal CyALT lifetimes under interval monitoring, based on the weighted minimum density power divergence estimator. Both tests are derived for simple and composite null hypotheses. A Monte Carlo study shows that classical tests lose control of the significance level rapidly under contamination, while the robust tests stay close to nominal with only a modest loss of power under clean data. An application to air-conditioner evaporator lifetime data further illustrates the proposed tests.

</details>


### [294] [Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects from randomised trials: a comparison of the risk modelling and treatment effect modelling approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21526)
*Frederik Luca Philipona,Marta Mainetti,Orestis Efthimiou,Georgia Salanti*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从理论和模拟两方面比较了风险建模（RM）与治疗效果建模（EM）两种估计异质性治疗效果的方法。结果显示，在多数模拟情境中RM表现优于EM，即使RM的前提假设不完全满足；但随样本量增大，RM的优势逐渐减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RM与EM是从随机对照试验估计异质性治疗效果的两类建模方法，各有理论优缺点，但学界一直缺乏包含模拟研究的系统比较，因此需要厘清两方法的前提假设与实际表现差异。

Method: 先对两种方法进行理论综述，阐明各自的假设与理论优劣；据此设计二分类结局RCT的模拟情境，以预测风险差的均方根误差和偏倚为指标，评估并比较RM与EM的性能。

Result: 理论分析表明，基线风险在多数临床情境中是治疗效果的效应修饰因子；RM是一种降维方法，但对预后因素如何修饰疗效作了较强假设，EM更灵活但需较大样本。在多数模拟情境中RM表现优于EM，即便RM假设不完全成立；随着样本量增大，RM的相对优势减弱。

Conclusion: 在选择RM还是EM时，应综合考虑可用样本量大小以及两方法前提假设的合理性。

Abstract: Introduction
  Risk modelling (RM) and treatment effect modelling (EM) are two approaches to build models to estimate heterogeneous treatment effects from randomized control trials (RCT). RM is a two-stage approach; it estimates a predicted baseline risk at the first stage and then includes it as the only effect modifier in a regression model in the second stage. EM is a full treatment interaction multivariable model. Both approaches have theoretical advantages and limitations, but a thorough comparison including a simulation study is missing.
  Methods
  In a theoretical review of the two approaches, we present their underlying assumptions and theoretical advantages and disadvantages. Based on our theoretical review, we design simulation scenarios for RCTs with a dichotomous outcome and evaluate the performance of both approaches with respect to the root mean square error and the bias in the predicted risk difference.
  Results
  In the theoretical part we argue that baseline risk is a treatment effect modifier in many clinical situations. RM is a dimensionality reduction approach which, however, makes strong assumptions about the role of prognostic factors modifying the treatment effect. EM's greater flexibility is a possible advantage when the sample size is large. In most simulated scenarios RM performs better than EM, even when the assumptions underlying RM are not fully met. The advantage of RM diminishes as sample size increases.
  Conclusion
  When choosing between RM and EM the available sample size and the plausibility of their underlying assumptions should be considered.

</details>


### [295] [Fuzzy entropic triple k-means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21538)
*Mariaelena Bottazzi Schenone,Roberto Rocci,Maurizio Vichi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出模糊熵三元k均值（FE3KM），一种对三向数据阵列同时聚类对象、变量和场合的熵正则化模糊划分方法，其线性隶属结构支持总偏差的精确分解，并通过模拟研究和一个电视收视率数据集应用验证了其有效性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模糊聚类通过非线性指数m控制模糊性，无法将总偏差精确分解为类内与类间成分；三向数据（对象×变量×场合）的同步聚类也缺乏具有这一可解释分解性质的方法。作者希望用线性隶属（行随机矩阵）加熵正则化取代非线性指数，从而在保持模糊不确定性的同时获得对三个模式及每个簇的精确方差贡献分解。

Method: 提出FE3KM：在最小二乘（LS）目标中将隶属度线性嵌入，采用行随机隶属矩阵并用熵项正则化以表示不确定度；推导交替最小二乘（ALS）算法，并针对大型阵列给出加速变体；将该方法形式化地与串联聚类（tandem clustering）及Tucker3/三模式划分模型建立联系；通过模拟研究评估恢复精度、对噪声和模糊度误设的鲁棒性，并与串联及三向聚类基线对比。

Result: FE3KM的线性隶属结构实现了总偏差（total deviance）向类内、类间成分的精确分解，且可进一步归因于三个模式中的每个模式及每个簇，这是指数m模糊模型所不具备的；模拟研究表明其在恢复精度、噪声鲁棒性和模糊度误设条件下表现良好，并优于串联与三向聚类基线；在基准三向电视收视率数据上恢复了可解释的对象-变量-场合结构，并量化了各簇与各模式对总体变异性的贡献。

Conclusion: FE3KM通过线性隶属与熵正则化，为三向数据提供了一种兼具模糊性和精确偏差分解能力的同步聚类方法，克服了指数m模糊模型在可解释分解上的局限，并通过模拟与应用验证了其有效性、鲁棒性与实用性。

Abstract: This paper proposes fuzzy entropic triple k-means (FE3KM), an entropy-regularized fuzzy partitioning method for three-way data arrays that simultaneously clusters objects, variables, and occasions. Unlike standard fuzzy clustering, which controls fuzziness through a nonlinear exponent m, FE3KM embeds memberships linearly within a Least-Squares (LS) objective, using row-stochastic membership matrices and entropy regularization to represent uncertainty in cluster assignments. This linear structure is what enables an exact decomposition of total deviance into within- and between-cluster components, further attributable to each mode (objects, variables, occasions) and to individual clusters within each mode. This is an interpretive property unavailable to exponent-m fuzzy models. We derive Alternating Least-Squares algorithms for FE3KM, including an accelerated variant for large arrays, and formally relate the method to tandem clustering procedures and to Tucker3/three-mode partitioning models. A simulation study assesses recovery accuracy, robustness to noise and fuzziness mis-specification, and performance against tandem and three-way clustering baselines. An application to a benchmark three-way TV ratings dataset demonstrates how FE3KM recovers interpretable object-variable-occasion structures and quantifies the contribution of each cluster and mode to overall data variability.

</details>


### [296] [Model-based estimation and imputation with torus missing values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21549)
*Luca Greco,Lucia Filippozzi,Claudio Agostinelli*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对存在缺失值的p维环面（torus）多元圆形数据，提出基于EM算法的极大似然估计与模型插补框架，重点研究多元环绕正态分布，把环绕系数和缺失项同时视为潜变量进行处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆形数据的样本空间具有周期性特征，使得面向欧氏空间设计的传统插补技术不再适用，因此需要专门针对环面（多维圆形）数据的参数估计与缺失值插补方法。

Method: 在可忽略缺失机制下，提出环绕椭圆对称分布族（重点为多元环绕正态分布）的极大似然估计通用框架。利用展开空间中椭圆对称分布的条件性质，将环绕系数与缺失项同时作为潜变量，嵌入EM算法中完成缺失环面数据的插补与参数估计，并详细推导了E步与M步，给出了可实际运行的算法及插补方法。

Result: 在环绕正态设定下，通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估了可忽略缺失机制下极大似然估计量的有限样本表现，并在人为引入缺失值的数据上展示了该方法的应用效果。

Conclusion: 所提EM框架能够有效处理环面数据在可忽略缺失机制下的参数估计与缺失值插补问题，克服了传统欧氏插补方法因样本空间周期性而失效的局限。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of parameter estimation and model-based imputation for multivariate circular data lying on a p-dimensional torus in the presence of missing values. Actually, the periodic nature of the sample space invalidates conventional imputation techniques designed for Euclidean data. Then, we propose a general framework for maximum likelihood estimation under the wrapped elliptically symmetric family of distributions, with particular interest in the multivariate wrapped normal distribution, when the missing data mechanism is ignorable. The methodology leverages the conditional properties of the elliptically symmetric distributions on the unwrapped space, embedding the imputation of missing torus data into an Expectation-Maximization algorithm that treats both the wrapping coefficients and the missing entries as latent variables. Derivation of both the E and M steps is detailed and a working algorithm is discussed. Imputation methods are also taken into account. The finite-sample performance of the maximum likelihood estimator under ignorable missingness is assessed through Monte Carlo simulations under the wrapped normal specification. The methodology is also illustrated on data with artificially introduced missingness.

</details>


### [297] [Phasing out Monte Carlo: exact acceptance probabilities for mean-spread release rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21577)
*Remus Osan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二维傅里叶反演的方法，对以（样本均值, 样本标准差）落入固定区域为准则的放行决策（如含量均匀度 USP <905>、能力放行等），在任意平滑母体分布下精确计算验收概率，避免了正态假设和蒙特卡洛模拟的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类放行决策的验收概率取决于 (X̄, s) 的联合有限样本分布，而该分布对一般母体是一个受约束的 n 重积分（Craig 1932 对 n=3,4；Springer 1953 对一般 n），没有初等化简。当前实践中往往依赖正态假设或模拟，然而真实概率实际上由加性对 (T1,T2)=(ΣXi, ΣXi²) 的分布决定，其母函数是单观测变换的 n 次幂，为精确计算提供了可操作的入口。

Method: 利用加性对 (T1,T2)=(Σ_{i=1}^n X_i, Σ_{i=1}^n X_i²) 的特征函数（单观测变换的 n 次幂），通过一次二维傅里叶反演，在维度恒为 2（与 n 无关）的网格上数值求解任意平滑母体下的验收概率。该恒等式是精确的，实际计算的是其有限网格反演，误差按各实例报告而非给出统一界。并以 n=3 的均匀母体解析解（X̄,s 联合分布、验收概率、能力分布）作为验证阶梯的锚点。

Result: 在 n=3 均匀母体下得到了闭式解；用其作为验证锚点后，在非正态母体下按要求样本量（n=10–30）端到端执行了三条规则。结果显示，相较于精确答案，基于正态理论的现有方法在各方案所针对的风险上产生足够大的偏差，且偏差在操作曲线上双向出现，无法用单一常数校正。

Conclusion: 正态理论下现有方法的误差足以改变方案所设计防范的风险，且沿操作曲线双向偏斜，无法用单一常数修正；在一般情况下只有完整分布才能准确刻画验收概率，本文的二维傅里叶反演方法为任意平滑母体提供了一种确定性的精确替代方案。

Abstract: A large family of regulated release decisions accepts a production lot if and only if the pair (sample mean, sample standard deviation) of $n$ units falls in a fixed plane region. Content uniformity (USP <905>), capability release, variables sampling and percent-within-limits highway acceptance all take this form. The acceptance probability under a hypothesized unit-level law is not, so far as we are aware, evaluated exactly in current practice: for a general parent the joint finite-$n$ law of $(\bar X,s)$ is a constrained $n$-fold integral (Craig 1932 at $n=3,4$; Springer 1953 at general $n$) which admits no elementary reduction. Yet that probability is fixed by the law of the additive pair $(T_1,T_2)=(\sum_{i=1}^{n} X_i,\sum_{i=1}^{n} X_i^2)$, whose characteristic function is the $n$-th power of a one-observation transform. One two-dimensional Fourier inversion therefore delivers it for an arbitrary smooth parent - exactly, as an identity, and numerically on a grid whose dimension stays two whatever $n$ is - a deterministic alternative to both the simulation and the Normality assumption. The identity is exact; what we evaluate is a finite-grid inversion of it, whose error we report per example rather than bound. At $n=3$ the Uniform parent yields the joint law of $(\bar X,s)$, the acceptance probability and the capability law in closed form; we derive these, use them as the anchor of a validation ladder, and then work three rules end to end under non-normal parents at their prescribed sample sizes ($n=10$-$30$). Against the exact answer the Normal-theory incumbent errs by enough to change the risk a plan is designed against, and errs in both directions along the operating curve, so no single constant corrects it. In general only the full distribution suffices.

</details>


### [298] [Testing Conditional Stochastic Dominance via Copula Derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21622)
*Weiqi Yang,Weiwei Zhuang,Xiaojun Song*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在存在不同协变量边缘分布的情况下,比较两总体在相同协变量取值处整个条件分布的方法,并将其转化为对一阶随机占优的同时性检验证据。该方法通过总体特定边缘映射和copula导数表示将条件分布跨区域连接,并通过单侧统计量进行一致推断,在有限copula类正确设定等条件下实现均匀控制与一致校准。模拟与PSID应用验证了方法的有效性及敏感性和样本量偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较两总体在相同物理协变量值处的差异时,仅依赖条件均值或孤立目标点决策不够充分,研究者可能需要关于整个条件分布序关系的证据,即使协变量边缘分布不同。本文旨在使这种共同值比较在明确的结构-灵活性权衡下可估计,并将结果表面对应为一阶随机占优的同时性证据。

Method: 利用总体特定边缘分布将共同协变量值映射到各组,并用拟合的copula导数表示跨区域的连接条件分布;通过未知约束位置的单侧统计量,对边缘模型和相依模型的不确定性进行一致推断。在有限copula类内正确设定、光滑性与截断条件、以及唯一最优候选族等条件下,该方法具备均匀控制和一致校准。

Result: 模拟显示当备择假设更加可区分时拒绝率提升,同时暴露出模型选择敏感性与小样本规模扭曲;在描述性PSID应用中,高-低父母教育程度比较在经过多重性调整后满足双向标准,而相邻教育组比较仍无定论。

Conclusion: 该框架支持区域范围的分布比较,同时明确其结构与推断边界,为在边缘分布不同情形下进行条件分布序检验提供了可行且透明的工具。

Abstract: Comparing two populations at the same physical covariate value requires more than conditional means or isolated target-point decisions: researchers may need evidence about an entire conditional-distribution ordering over a continuum, even when covariate margins differ. This paper makes that common-value comparison estimable under an explicit structure--flexibility tradeoff and turns the resulting surface into simultaneous evidence for first-order stochastic dominance. Population-specific margins map the common covariate value into each group, while a fitted copula-derivative representation links conditional distributions across the region. Uniform inference propagates uncertainty from both the margins and dependence model through a one-sided statistic with unknown binding locations. Under correct specification within a finite copula class, smoothness and trimming conditions, and a uniquely best candidate family, the procedure admits uniform control and consistent calibration. Simulations show increasing rejection as alternatives become more distinguishable, alongside model-selection sensitivity and small-sample size distortion. In a descriptive PSID application, the high--low parental-education comparison satisfies the two-direction criterion after multiplicity adjustment, whereas adjacent education-group comparisons remain inconclusive. The framework therefore supports region-wide distributional comparison while making its structural and inferential boundaries explicit.

</details>


### [299] [A Partial Fay-Herriot model for Small Area Estimation: Estimating district-level consumption in Mozambique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21701)
*Francesco Schirripa Spagnolo,Nicola Salvati,Enrico Fabrizi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出Partial Fay-Herriot(PFH)估计量，将偏最小二乘(PLS)融入Fay-Herriot模型，以应对高维且高度相关的辅助变量集，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟及莫桑比克地区人均消费估计验证其优于标准FH估计量和基于主成分的替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小区域估计中Fay-Herriot模型常面临辅助变量数量过多且高度相关的问题，导致模型不稳定、估计精度下降；传统的基于主成分(无监督)的降维方法未利用目标变量信息，降维方向可能与目标关联较弱，因此需要一种监督式降维方法以提升模型稳定性与预测效率。

Method: 提出Partial Fay-Herriot(PFH)估计方法，将偏最小二乘(PLS)集成到Fay-Herriot模型中，通过构造与目标变量关联最大化的监督成分进行降维；采用蒙特卡洛模拟将其与标准Fay-Herriot估计量及基于主成分的替代方法进行对比，并将方法应用于莫桑比克区级人均消费估计(辅以大量相关的人口普查变量)。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟表明，PFH估计量的均方误差低于标准Fay-Herriot估计量，并优于基于主成分的替代方法，且仅需更少的潜在维度；莫桑比克应用结果显示估计值呈现出显著的地理异质性，并揭示了剥夺的空间聚集模式。

Conclusion: 所提出的监督式降维方法(PLS-FH)在高维情境下是一种有效且易于解释的工具，能够生成可靠的指标，为小区域估计的高维相关问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new small area estimation approach that integrates Partial Least Squares within the Fay-Herriot model to address the challenges posed by high-dimensional and highly correlated auxiliary variables sets. The resulting Partial Fay-Herriot (PFH) estimator constructs supervised components that maximize their association with the target variable, enhancing model stability and predictive efficiency. Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate that PFH estimator achieves lower mean squared error than the standard Fay-Herriot estimator and outperforms principal components-based alternatives while relying on fewer latent dimensions. The methodology is applied to the estimation of district-level per capita consumption in Mozambique, where the survey data source is complemented by large set of correlated census variables. The resulting estimates highlight pronounced geographic heterogeneity and uncover spatial clusters of deprivation. Overall, the findings show that the proposed supervised dimension-reduction approach represents an effective and easily interpretable tool for producing reliable indicators in high-dimensional contexts.

</details>


### [300] [Optimal study interior partitioning (OSIP): An optimization approach to observational study design under fine balance constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21902)
*Ron Adar,Asaf Levin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为 OSIP（最优研究内部划分）的新型观察性因果推断匹配方法，将其建模为受最大层数约束与严格精细平衡阈值共同控制的联合优化问题，并通过两阶段算法（先按倾向得分进行一维最优划分，再局部启发式优化），在多个基准数据集上验证其在平衡性与统计功效之间取得高效折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察性因果推断中，协变量匹配需在实现严格平衡与保持匹配样本的统计功效之间权衡；现有方法难以同时兼顾两者，研究者亟需一种能系统求解该多目标折中问题的方法。

Method: 提出 OSIP 框架，将匹配建模为受层数最大基数约束与每层严格精细平衡阈值共同约束的联合优化问题。方法采用两阶段算法：先通过估计的倾向得分将高维数据投影到一维空间，对连续倾向得分区间进行全局最优划分（第一阶段）；再回到原始距离函数，用启发式方法对第一阶段解进行局部优化（第二阶段）。

Result: 在 Lindner 心血管数据集这一标准基准上，OSIP 为对应的多准则目标提供了高效的"甜点"解；这一结论在其他基准数据集（如 lalonde、nsw 等）上得到进一步验证。

Conclusion: OSIP 通过全局最优划分与局部优化相结合的两阶段策略，有效解决了平衡性与统计功效之间的权衡难题，在多个基准上表现出优于既有方法的综合性能，为应用研究者提供了一种高效实用的匹配工具。

Abstract: Covariate matching between treatment and control groups remains a foundational methodology in observational causal inference, designed to replicate the covariate balance of randomized controlled trials. Despite substantial methodological advancements, applied researchers continue to face an inherent trade-off between achieving rigorous balance and preserving the statistical power of the matched sample. We introduce a novel framework for a matching method named Optimal Study Interior Partitioning (OSIP). OSIP frames observational matching as a joint optimization problem governed simultaneously by a maximum cardinality constraint on the number of strata, and a strict fine balance threshold on the matching carried out in each strata. OSIP is based on two-stage optimization algorithm. In both stages it considers a projection of the (high-dimensional) data into one-dimensional space by evaluating an estimated propensity score for each unit. The method is based on enforcing a partition of the input to subsets of consecutive intervals of estimated propensity scores. The novelty arises because we consider this task as a global optimization problem. In the first stage, we employ an optimal partitioning algorithm with respect to an auxiliary (one-dimensional) goal function. In the second phase, we go back to the original distance function and apply heuristics approaches locally optimizing the solution from the first stage. We compare our framework with established methodologies from the literature across well-studied empirical benchmarks. We use the standard benchmark of Lindner cardiovascular dataset to demonstrate that OSIP provides a highly efficient sweet spot solution for the corresponding multi-criteria goal. This conclusion is verified by considering also other benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [301] [Consistency of Optimal Matching-based Clustering for Mixtures of Markov Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21985)
*Ottavio Khalifa*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限状态马尔可夫链有限混合模型下，基于最优匹配（OM）距离对类别序列进行聚类，证明了归一化 OM 距离的几乎必然收敛性、指数集中性及其收敛速率，并据此给出聚类一致性与成分数估计的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OM 距离是生命历程与序列分析中广泛使用的距离度量，但其在马尔可夫链混合模型下的统计性质缺乏理论支撑，导致聚类方法与成分数的选择缺乏统计学依据。

Method: 在有限状态马尔可夫链的有限混合模型下，研究归一化 OM 距离的渐近性质：证明两条独立链的归一化 OM 距离几乎必然收敛到确定的总体量，并围绕有限时域均值指数集中、具有 O(√(log n/n)) 的收敛速率；在此基础上建立"最大类内限值小于最小类间限值"的分离条件，并分析括号型 linkage 的层次聚类与 PAM 的一致性，以及基于经验 OM 距离轮廓的成分数估计量。

Result: 归一化 OM 距离几乎必然收敛到确定的总体量，并指数集中且收敛速率为 O(√(log n/n))（链具有相同转移核时）；在分离条件下，任意括号型 linkage 的层次聚类与 PAM 一致地恢复潜在混合划分；成分数估计量具有一致性；结果可推广到有限状态隐马尔可夫模型与多通道类别观测。

Conclusion: 本文为标准 OM 聚类方法在类别时间序列中的应用提供了统计合理性，并证明了其在混合马尔可夫链模型下的一致性，同时将结论推广至隐马尔可夫模型与多通道观测。

Abstract: We study clustering of categorical sequences using the Optimal Matching (OM) distance under finite mixtures of finite-state Markov chains. We show that the normalized OM distance between two independent chains converges almost surely to a deterministic population quantity, concentrates exponentially around its finite-horizon mean, and admits an $O(\sqrt{\log n/n})$ convergence rate when the two chains have the same transition kernel. These population quantities yield a natural separation condition: the largest within-component limit must be smaller than the smallest between-component limit. Under this condition, hierarchical clustering with any bracketed linkage and Partitioning Around Medoids consistently recover the latent mixture partition. We also propose a consistent estimator of the number of components based on empirical OM distance profiles. The results extend to finite-state hidden Markov models and multichannel categorical observations. Overall, they provide a statistical justification for standard OM-based clustering methods for categorical time series.

</details>


### [302] [Integrating Multivariate Adaptive Regression Splines into Small Area Estimation for Nonlinear Poverty Modeling in Java Island](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21987)
*Ajriansyah,Bambang Widjanarko Otok,Sutikno*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出将多元自适应回归样条（MARS）整合进小区域估计（SAE）框架，以解决传统Fay-Herriot模型无法处理非线性模式的问题，并在印度尼西亚爪哇岛的贫困数据上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小区域估计常用于解决样本量有限地区估计值不可靠的问题，但标准SAE模型（如Fay-Herriot）只能适应线性模式，而现实中（包括贫困问题）大量模式是非线性的，因此需要一种能够刻画非线性关系且保持可解释性的SAE方法。

Method: 在SAE框架中集成监督学习模型MARS（MARS-SAE），通过超参数调优确定最优模型：最大基函数数=10、最大交互阶数=2、最小观测数=1、惩罚项=3，对应广义交叉验证（GCV）=6.128。并以SAE Fay-Herriot作为对比模型进行评估。

Result: MARS-SAE表现出很强的性能：相对效率（RE）更高，能更有效地降低估计误差；相对标准误（RSE）降低幅度优于对比模型；相对均方根误差（RRMSE）低于Fay-Herriot模型（13.64% vs. 13.75%）。

Conclusion: MARS-SAE能够很好地捕捉非线性模式，同时保持可解释性，是现象预测与诊断分析的有效工具。由于爪哇岛是印尼——东盟最大经济体——的战略区域，本研究可作为区域及东南亚读者的重要参考。

Abstract: Small area estimation (SAE) modeling is often used to address the unreliability of estimates in areas with limited survey samples. However, standard SAE models (Fay-Herriot) can only accommodate linear patterns, whereas in real cases, including poverty, many patterns are nonlinear. This study proposes the implementation of a supervised learning model, multivariate adaptive regression splines (MARS), integrated within the SAE framework (MARS-SAE) to address the challenge of unreliable direct estimation, particularly in nonlinear cases. The hyperparameter tuning results yielded the best model with the following hyperparameter combinations: maximum basis function (Max BF) = 10, maximum interaction (MI) = 2, minimum observation (MO) = 1, penalty = 3, with general cross validation (GCV) = 6.128. MARS-SAE demonstrated very strong performance; empirically, the model proved more efficient at reducing estimation error due to its higher relative efficiency (RE) compared to the SAE Fay-Herriot model, as well as better reduction of relative standard error (RSE) than the comparison model, and it yielded a lower relative root mean squared error (RRMSE) compared to the SAE Fay-Herriot model (13.64% vs. 13.75%). MARS-SAE has been proven to capture nonlinear patterns very well while still maintaining interpretability, making it an effective tool for the predictive and diagnostic analyses of phenomena. Thus, this research can serve as an important reference for readers regionally and in Southeast Asia, given that Java Island is a strategic region for Indonesia, the largest economic power in the ASEAN region.

</details>


### [303] [A Novel Convolution-Based Stratified Attribute Estimator for QRE Determination in R&D Tax Credit Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21998)
*Deborah Lynn Goldwasser*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟研究比较了属性抽样与变量抽样两类统计方法在合格研究支出（QRE）估算中的表现，并提出一种基于卷积的分层属性估计器，能给出可靠的95%单侧置信下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 《One Big, Beautiful Bill Act》恢复了国内研究支出的立即费用化（immediate expensing），激励企业在美研发投入，使得准确且可辩护地估算合格研究支出（QRE）变得愈发重要。IRS Rev. Proc. 2011-42 允许属性与变量两类统计方法，但现有实践通常将分层均值/差异估计（变量法）视为标准，缺乏对属性法优势的系统论证，且简单的属性估计器存在因样本框中大量高潜在QRE（pQRE）非合格项目导致向上偏误的风险。

Method: 作者在模拟研究框架下对属性法与变量法（分层均值与差异估计量）进行比较：1）论证属性法在理论与实务上的多项优势；2）针对简单属性估计器的向上偏误问题，引入一种分层抽样设计，确保高pQRE项目在样本中获得充分代表；3）构建基于卷积的分层属性估计器，用以给出总体QRE的95%单侧置信下界。

Result: 模拟结果表明，属性方法相对变量方法具有理论与实务优势；所提出的分层抽样设计有效缓解了高pQRE非合格项目引发的向上偏误；卷积型分层属性估计器在多种样本框结构下均能产生有效的95%单侧置信下界。

Conclusion: 本文证明属性统计方法可作为QRE估算的有力替代方案，通过分层设计与卷积估计器克服其偏误缺陷，为满足IRC第41节（Form 6765）要求的QRE估算提供了一种准确、可辩护且稳健的统计框架。

Abstract: The One Big, Beautiful Bill Act reinstates immediate expensing of domestic research expenses, reducing the tax burden and incentivizing reinvestment in U.S.-based research and development including software development across a wide range of industries. In this context, accurate and defensible estimation of qualified research expenses (QREs) is of high importance. Statistical sampling provides a practical framework for estimating QREs for a well-defined population of business components (sampling frame) documented in accordance with Internal Revenue Code Section 41 (Form 6765). IRS Revenue Procedure 2011-42 permits both attribute and variable statistical methods, although the latter (stratified mean and difference estimators) are often regarded as the standard approach to QRE estimation. In this paper, we compare attribute and variable statistical methods within a simulation study and demonstrate that attribute methods offer several theoretical and practical advantages. A key concern with the simple attribute estimator is the possibility of upward bias in QRE determination arising from a preponderance of high potential QRE (pQRE), non-qualified projects in the sampling frame. We address this concern by introducing a stratified sampling design that ensures adequate representation of high pQRE projects in the sample. We demonstrate that a convolution-based stratified attribute estimator produces a valid one-sided 95% lower confidence bound on total QREs across a range of sampling frame structures.

</details>


### [304] [RKHS-Based Inference for Nonlinear Granger Causality via Conditional Centering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22007)
*Yuhan Tian,Adam Waterbury,Marie-Christine Düker*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）的非线性格兰杰非因果性检验，通过条件中心化分解与核岭回归，构造具有加权卡方零极限分布且无需重采样的谱校准检验统计量，在模拟与真实数据中均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统格兰杰因果性通过向量自回归模型的线性预测来表述，难以捕捉变量之间的非线性预测关系，因此需要发展适用于非线性自回归过程的非因果性检验方法。

Method: 将目标回归函数分解为“自身历史分量”与正交的“源历史增量贡献分量”，非因果性等价于后者为零。先用核岭回归估计自身历史分量，再在第二个RKHS中用条件中心化核嵌入残差，得到RKHS取值的残差矩，以其平方范数作为检验统计量。进一步构造经验中心化版本，其零极限分布为加权卡方，可通过谱校准获得临界值与p值，无需重采样。

Result: 对总体中心化统计量建立了加权卡方零极限，并证明对固定备择假设具有一致性；经验中心化版本保持相同的零极限，从而支持谱校准。模拟研究表明该方法尺寸控制准确、对非线性备择具有检验功效，真实数据应用显示了其在时间序列非线性预测关系检测中的实用性。

Conclusion: 所提RKHS框架能够有效检验非线性格兰杰非因果性，兼具理论保证（加权卡方零极限、一致性）与计算便利（无需重采样的谱校准），在模拟与实证中均验证了其有效性，可用于检测时间序列中的非线性预测关系。

Abstract: Granger causality is commonly formulated through linear prediction in vector autoregressive models, limiting its ability to detect nonlinear predictive relationships. We propose a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS)-based test for nonlinear Granger non-causality in conditional mean for nonlinear autoregressive processes. The key idea is a conditional-centering decomposition of the target regression function into an own-history component and an orthogonal component capturing the additional predictive contribution of the potential source history. Non-causality is characterized by the vanishing of the latter component. The own-history component is estimated by kernel ridge regression, and the residuals are embedded in a second RKHS using a conditionally centered kernel. This yields an RKHS-valued residual moment whose squared norm forms the test statistic. We establish a weighted chi-square null limit and consistency against fixed alternatives for the population-centered statistic. An empirically centered version is shown to retain the null limit and enables spectral calibration of critical values and $p$-values without resampling. Simulation studies demonstrate accurate size control and power against nonlinear alternatives, and real-data applications illustrate the usefulness of the method for detecting nonlinear predictive relationships in time series.

</details>
